## MODEL ✨

Based on your current codebase, here are several feature ideas categorized by complexity and impact. Which direction would you like to explore?

### 1. Essential / Practical Additions
*   **Availability Calendar:** Currently, bookings rely on manual date/time entry. Implement a calendar view where instructors can define "working hours" or specific blocks of availability, preventing double-booking.
*   **Payment Integration:** Add a "Payment Status" to bookings. You could simulate a payment gateway (like Stripe) where students must pay a deposit to confirm a booking.
*   **Notifications:** Send simple email or simulated SMS alerts when a booking status changes (Pending -> Accepted) or when a new message arrives.

### 2. Educational Enhancements
*   **Practice Quizzes:** Add a module for "Theory Test Preparation" with multiple-choice questions. You already have an `education_book.html`, so a quiz section would fit naturally.
*   **Progress Analytics:** Visualize the `DrivingSession` data. Create a chart for students showing their most common faults (e.g., "Speed" or "Observation") over time so they know what to focus on.

### 3. User Experience (UX)
*   **Route Mapping:** Allow instructors to (manually or via a map interface) pin the "Start" and "End" locations of a lesson, visualizing the pickup/drop-off points on a map (using Leaflet or Google Maps).
*   **Instructor Verification:** Enhance the mock verification flow by allowing instructors to upload a document image that an admin (new role) has to "approve."

### 4. Community / Trust
*   **Badges/Achievements:** Award badges to students for milestones (e.g., "10 Hours Logged", "Night Owl" for evening drives, "Safe Driver" for a session with 0 faults).
*   **Referral System:** Allow students to invite friends and earn credits or discounts.

**My Recommendation:**
**Availability Calendar** or **Progress Analytics** would add the most immediate value to your current "Driving School" platform.

How would you like to proceed?

## MODEL ✨

That's great! Since we have a lot of good options, I recommend we tackle them in a logical order.

I suggest we start with the **Availability Calendar**. It's the "backbone" of a booking app—students need to know *when* an instructor is free before they can book effectively.

Here is the plan to implement it:

1.  **Database Update:**
    *   Create a new table `InstructorAvailability` to store recurring weekly slots (e.g., "Monday 09:00 - 17:00").
    *   Link it to the `InstructorProfile`.

2.  **Instructor Dashboard:**
    *   Add a simple form where instructors can add their working hours (Day of Week + Start Time + End Time).
    *   Display a list of their current working hours.

3.  **Booking Logic (Backend):**
    *   Update the booking creation endpoint to check if the requested time falls within the instructor's availability.
    *   (For now, we will keep it simple: strict checking against these slots).

4.  **Student Booking Experience:**
    *   We'll start by ensuring the backend validates the time. (Visualizing this on a calendar UI is a larger task we can do in a "Part 2").

**Shall I proceed with setting up the Database and Instructor Dashboard changes for Availability?**

---\ndef expand_codes(code_string: Optional[str]) -> str:\n    if not code_string:\n        return \"\"\n    codes = [c.strip() for c in code_string.split(',') if c.strip()]\n    expanded = []\n    for code in codes:\n        desc = FAULT_MAP.get(code, code)\n        expanded.append(f\"{code} - {desc}\")\n    return \", \".join(expanded)\n\ntemplates.env.filters[\"expand_codes\"] = expand_codes\n\n# Dependency\ndef get_db():\n    db = database.SessionLocal()\n    try:\n        yield db\n    finally:\n        db.close()\n\n# --- Dummy Auth Logic (Cookies for simplicity in prototype) ---\ndef get_current_user_id(request: Request):\n    return request.cookies.get(\"user_id\")\n\ndef get_current_user(request: Request, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):\n    user_id = get_current_user_id(request)\n    if not user_id:\n        return None\n    try:\n        uid = int(user_id)\n        return db.query(models.User).filter(models.User.id == uid).first()\n    except (ValueError, TypeError):\n        return None\n\ndef get_unread_count(db: Session, user: models.User) -> int:\n    if not user: return 0\n    return db.query(models.Message).filter(\n        models.Message.recipient_id == user.id,\n        models.Message.is_read == False\n    ).count()\n\n# --- Routes ---\n\n@app.get(\"/education\", response_class=HTMLResponse)\nasync def education_home(request: Request, user: models.User = Depends(get_current_user), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\"education_home.html\", {\n        \"request\": request,\n        \"user\": user,\n        \"unread_count\": get_unread_count(db, user) if user else 0\n    })\n\n@app.get(\"/education/bc\", response_class=HTMLResponse)\nasync def education_book_bc(request: Request, user: models.User = Depends(get_current_user), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\"education_book.html\", {\n        \"request\": request,\n        \"user\": user,\n        \"unread_count\": get_unread_count(db, user) if user else 0\n    })\n\n@app.get(\"/\", response_class=HTMLResponse)\nasync def read_root(request: Request, user: models.User = Depends(get_current_user), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):\n    unread = 0\n    if user:\n        try:\n            unread = get_unread_count(db, user)\n        except Exception:\n            pass\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\"home.html\", {\"request\": request, \"user\": user, \"unread_count\": unread})\n\n@app.get(\"/login\", response_class=HTMLResponse)\nasync def login_page(request: Request):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\"login.html\", {\"request\": request})\n\n@app.post(\"/login\")\nasync def login(\n    request: Request,\n    email: str = Form(...),\n    password: str = Form(...),\n    db: Session = Depends(get_db)\n):\n    user = db.query(models.User).filter(models.User.email == email).first()\n    # Simple check, no hashing for prototype speed, meant to be replaced by real auth\n    if not user or user.hashed_password != password:\n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\"login.html\", {\"request\": request, \"error\": \"Invalid credentials\"})\n    \n    response = RedirectResponse(url=\"/dashboard\", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)\n    response.set_cookie(key=\"user_id\", value=str(user.id))\n    return response\n\n@app.get(\"/logout\")\nasync def logout(request: Request):\n    response = RedirectResponse(url=\"/\", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)\n    response.delete_cookie(\"user_id\")\n    return response\n\n@app.get(\"/forgot-password\", response_class=HTMLResponse)\nasync def forgot_password_page(request: Request):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\"forgot_password.html\", {\"request\": request})\n\n@app.post(\"/forgot-password\", response_class=HTMLResponse)\nasync def forgot_password(\n    request: Request,\n    email: str = Form(...),\n    db: Session = Depends(get_db)\n):\n    user = db.query(models.User).filter(models.User.email == email).first()\n    if user:\n        token = str(uuid.uuid4())\n        user.reset_token = token\n        # Expire in 1 hour\n        user.reset_token_expiry = (datetime.now() + timedelta(hours=1)).isoformat()\n        db.commit()\n        \n        # Simulate sending email\n        reset_link = f\"{request.base_url}reset-password?token={token}\"\n        print(f\"PASSWORD RESET LINK for {email}: {reset_link}\") # Log to console\n        \n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\"forgot_password.html\", {\"request\": request, \"message\": \"If an account exists for this email, a reset link has been sent.\"})\n\n@app.get(\"/reset-password\", response_class=HTMLResponse)\nasync def reset_password_page(request: Request, token: str):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\"reset_password.html\", {\"request\": request, \"token\": token})\n\n@app.post(\"/reset-password\", response_class=HTMLResponse)\nasync def reset_password(\n    request: Request,\n    token: str = Form(...),\n    password: str = Form(...),\n    confirm_password: str = Form(...),\n    db: Session = Depends(get_db)\n):\n    if password != confirm_password:\n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\"reset_password.html\", {\"request\": request, \"token\": token, \"error\": \"Passwords do not match\"})\n        \n    user = db.query(models.User).filter(models.User.reset_token == token).first()\n    \n    if not user:\n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\"reset_password.html\", {\"request\": request, \"token\": token, \"error\": \"Invalid or expired token\"})\n        \n    # Check expiry\n    try:\n        expiry = datetime.fromisoformat(user.reset_token_expiry)\n        if datetime.now() > expiry:\n            return templates.TemplateResponse(\"reset_password.html\", {\"request\": request, \"token\": token, \"error\": \"Token expired\"})\n    except (ValueError, TypeError):\n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\"reset_password.html\", {\"request\": request, \"token\": token, \"error\": \"Invalid token expiry data\"})\n\n    # Update password\n    user.hashed_password = password # Still plain text per current implementation\n    user.reset_token = None\n    user.reset_token_expiry = None\n    db.commit()\n    \n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\"login.html\", {\"request\": request, \"message\": \"Password reset successful. Please login.\"})\n\n@app.get(\"/register\", response_class=HTMLResponse)\nasync def register_page(request: Request):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\"register.html\", {\"request\": request})\n\n@app.post(\"/register\")\nasync def register(\n    request: Request,\n    email: str = Form(...),\n    password: str = Form(...),\n    full_name: str = Form(...),\n    phone: str = Form(...),\n    role: str = Form(...),\n    db: Session = Depends(get_db)\n):\n    if db.query(models.User).filter(models.User.email == email).first():\n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\"register.html\", {\"request\": request, \"error\": \"Email already registered\"})\n    \n    new_user = models.User(\n        email=email,\n        hashed_password=password, # Use hashing in production\n        full_name=full_name,\n        phone_number=phone,\n        role=role\n    )\n    db.add(new_user)\n    db.commit()\n    db.refresh(new_user)\n    \n    # Redirect to profile setup\n    response = RedirectResponse(url=\"/setup-profile\", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)\n    response.set_cookie(key=\"user_id\", value=str(new_user.id))\n    return response\n\n@app.get(\"/setup-profile\", response_class=HTMLResponse)\nasync def setup_profile_page(request: Request, user: models.User = Depends(get_current_user), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):\n    if not user:\n        return RedirectResponse(url=\"/login\")\n    if user.role == \"instructor\":\n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\"setup_instructor.html\", {\"request\": request, \"user\": user, \"unread_count\": get_unread_count(db, user)})\n    else:\n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\"setup_student.html\", {\"request\": request, \"user\": user, \"unread_count\": get_unread_count(db, user)})\n\n@app.post(\"/setup-instructor\")\nasync def setup_instructor(\n    request: Request,\n    bio: str = Form(...),\n    hourly_rate: float = Form(...),\n    city: str = Form(...),\n    car_model: str = Form(...),\n    insurance_policy: str = Form(...),\n    certification_id: str = Form(...),\n    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n    user: models.User = Depends(get_current_user)\n):\n    if not user: return RedirectResponse(url=\"/login\")\n    \n    profile = models.InstructorProfile(\n        user_id=user.id,\n        bio=bio,\n        hourly_rate=hourly_rate,\n        city=city,\n        car_model=car_model,\n        insurance_policy=insurance_policy,\n        certification_id=certification_id,\n        is_verified=True # Auto-verify for prototype\n    )\n    db.add(profile)\n    db.commit()\n    return RedirectResponse(url=\"/dashboard\", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)\n\n@app.post(\"/setup-student\")\nasync def setup_student(\n    request: Request,\n    age: int = Form(...),\n    l_license_number: str = Form(...),\n    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n    user: models.User = Depends(get_current_user)\n):\n    if not user: return RedirectResponse(url=\"/login\")\n    \n    if age < 16:\n         return templates.TemplateResponse(\"setup_student.html\", {\"request\": request, \"user\": user, \"error\": \"You must be at least 16 years old.\"})\n\n    profile = models.StudentProfile(\n        user_id=user.id,\n        age=age,\n        l_license_number=l_license_number\n    )\n    db.add(profile)\n    db.commit()\n    return RedirectResponse(url=\"/dashboard\", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)\n\n@app.get(\"/dashboard\", response_class=HTMLResponse)\nasync def dashboard(\n    request: Request, \n    user: models.User = Depends(get_current_user),\n    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n    city: Optional[str] = None,\n    max_price: Optional[float] = None\n):\n    if not user:\n        return RedirectResponse(url=\"/login\")\n    \n    if user.role == \"student\":\n        # Fetch matching instructors\n        query = db.query(models.InstructorProfile).join(models.User).filter(models.InstructorProfile.is_available == True)\n        \n        if city:\n            query = query.filter(models.InstructorProfile.city.ilike(f\"%{city}%\"))\n        \n        if max_price:\n            query = query.filter(models.InstructorProfile.hourly_rate <= max_price)\n            \n        instructors = query.all()\n        \n        # Calculate ratings for display\n        for inst in instructors:\n            reviews = inst.reviews\n            if reviews:\n                avg = sum(r.rating for r in reviews) / len(reviews)\n                inst.average_rating = round(avg, 1)\n                inst.review_count = len(reviews)\n            else:\n                inst.average_rating = None\n                inst.review_count = 0\n\n        # Fetch my booking requests (All history)\n        all_bookings = db.query(models.BookingRequest).filter(models.BookingRequest.student_id == user.id)\\\n            .order_by(models.BookingRequest.date.desc(), models.BookingRequest.time.desc()).all()\n        \n        # Filter Active Bookings (Pending, Accepted, Cancellation Requested)\n        active_bookings = [b for b in all_bookings if b.status in ['pending', 'accepted', 'cancellation_requested']]\n        \n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\"student_dashboard.html\", {\n            \"request\": request, \n            \"user\": user, \n            \"instructors\": instructors,\n            \"active_bookings\": active_bookings,\n            \"filters\": {\"city\": city, \"max_price\": max_price},\n            \"unread_count\": get_unread_count(db, user)\n        })\n    \n    else: # Instructor\n        # Fetch all bookings\n        all_bookings = db.query(models.BookingRequest).filter(\n            models.BookingRequest.instructor_id == user.instructor_profile.id\n        ).order_by(models.BookingRequest.date.desc(), models.BookingRequest.time.desc()).all()\n        \n        active_bookings = [b for b in all_bookings if b.status in ['pending', 'accepted', 'cancellation_requested']]\n        \n        # Calculate my own rating\n        my_reviews = user.instructor_profile.reviews\n        if my_reviews:\n            avg_rating = sum(r.rating for r in my_reviews) / len(my_reviews)\n            avg_rating = round(avg_rating, 1)\n        else:\n            avg_rating = \"New\"\n\n        # Prepare Calendar Events (Accepted Bookings Only)\n        calendar_events = []\n        accepted_bookings = [b for b in all_bookings if b.status == 'accepted']\n        \n        for b in accepted_bookings:\n            try:\n                start_dt = datetime.strptime(f\"{b.date} {b.time}\", \"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M\")\n                end_dt = start_dt + timedelta(hours=b.duration)\n                calendar_events.append({\n                    \"title\": f\"Lesson with {b.student.full_name}\",\n                    \"start\": start_dt.isoformat(),\n                    \"end\": end_dt.isoformat(),\n                    \"color\": \"#198754\" # Bootstrap success green\n                })\n            except ValueError:\n                pass # Skip invalid dates\n\n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\"instructor_dashboard.html\", {\n            \"request\": request, \n            \"user\": user,\n            \"bookings\": active_bookings, # Renamed in template logic context, but variable name passed is what matters\n            \"avg_rating\": avg_rating,\n            \"review_count\": len(my_reviews),\n            \"calendar_events\": json.dumps(calendar_events),\n            \"unread_count\": get_unread_count(db, user)\n        })\n\n@app.get(\"/review/{instructor_id}\", response_class=HTMLResponse)\nasync def review_form(\n    request: Request, \n    instructor_id: int, \n    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n    user: models.User = Depends(get_current_user)\n):\n    if not user: return RedirectResponse(url=\"/login\")\n    \n    instructor = db.query(models.InstructorProfile).filter(models.InstructorProfile.id == instructor_id).first()\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\"review_form.html\", {\n        \"request\": request,\n        \"user\": user,\n        \"instructor\": instructor,\n        \"unread_count\": get_unread_count(db, user)\n    })\n\n@app.post(\"/review/{instructor_id}\")\nasync def submit_review(\n    request: Request,\n    instructor_id: int,\n    rating: int = Form(...),\n    comment: str = Form(...),\n    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n    user: models.User = Depends(get_current_user)\n):\n    if not user: return RedirectResponse(url=\"/login\")\n    \n    from datetime import datetime\n    \n    review = models.Review(\n        instructor_id=instructor_id,\n        student_id=user.id,\n        rating=rating,\n        comment=comment,\n        created_at=datetime.now().strftime(\"%Y-%m-%d\")\n    )\n    db.add(review)\n    db.commit()\n    \n    return RedirectResponse(url=\"/dashboard\", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)\n\n@app.post(\"/toggle-availability\")\nasync def toggle_availability(\n    request: Request, \n    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n    user: models.User = Depends(get_current_user)\n):\n    if user and user.instructor_profile:\n        user.instructor_profile.is_available = not user.instructor_profile.is_available\n        db.commit()\n    return RedirectResponse(url=\"/dashboard\", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)\n\n@app.get(\"/book/{instructor_id}\", response_class=HTMLResponse)\nasync def booking_form(\n    request: Request, \n    instructor_id: int, \n    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n    user: models.User = Depends(get_current_user)\n):\n    if not user: return RedirectResponse(url=\"/login\")\n    \n    instructor = db.query(models.InstructorProfile).filter(models.InstructorProfile.id == instructor_id).first()\n    if not instructor:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=\"Instructor not found\")\n\n    # Check for existing bookings with this instructor\n    existing_bookings = db.query(models.BookingRequest).filter(\n        models.BookingRequest.student_id == user.id,\n        models.BookingRequest.instructor_id == instructor_id,\n        models.BookingRequest.status.in_([\"pending\", \"accepted\"])\n    ).all()\n        \n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\"booking_form.html\", {\n        \"request\": request, \n        \"user\": user, \n        \"instructor\": instructor,\n        \"existing_bookings\": existing_bookings,\n        \"unread_count\": get_unread_count(db, user)\n    })\n            \n@app.post(\"/book/{instructor_id}\")\nasync def submit_booking(\n    request: Request,\n    instructor_id: int,\n    date: str = Form(...),\n    time: str = Form(...),\n    duration: int = Form(...),\n    pickup_address: str = Form(...),\n    notes: str = Form(\"\"),\n    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n    user: models.User = Depends(get_current_user)\n):\n    if not user: return RedirectResponse(url=\"/login\")\n    \n    # Validation for booking date and time\n    try:\n        new_start = datetime.strptime(f\"{date} {time}\", \"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M\")\n        current_time = datetime.now()\n        \n        instructor = db.query(models.InstructorProfile).filter(models.InstructorProfile.id == instructor_id).first()\n        existing_bookings = db.query(models.BookingRequest).filter(\n            models.BookingRequest.student_id == user.id,\n            models.BookingRequest.instructor_id == instructor_id,\n            models.BookingRequest.status.in_([\"pending\", \"accepted\"])\n        ).all()\n\n        if new_start < current_time:\n            return templates.TemplateResponse(\"booking_form.html\", {\n                \"request\": request,\n                \"user\": user,\n                \"instructor\": instructor,\n                \"existing_bookings\": existing_bookings,\n                \"error_message\": \"Booking date and time cannot be in the past.\",\n                \"unread_count\": get_unread_count(db, user)\n            })\n            \n        if new_start.date() == current_time.date() and new_start < current_time + timedelta(hours=2):\n            return templates.TemplateResponse(\"booking_form.html\", {\n                \"request\": request,\n                \"user\": user,\n                \"instructor\": instructor,\n                \"existing_bookings\": existing_bookings,\n                \"error_message\": \"Same-day bookings must be at least 2 hours in advance.\",\n                \"unread_count\": get_unread_count(db, user)\n            })\n\n    except ValueError:\n        # Handle parsing errors for date/time if necessary\n        instructor = db.query(models.InstructorProfile).filter(models.InstructorProfile.id == instructor_id).first()\n        existing_bookings = db.query(models.BookingRequest).filter(\n            models.BookingRequest.student_id == user.id,\n            models.BookingRequest.instructor_id == instructor_id,\n            models.BookingRequest.status.in_([\"pending\", \"accepted\"])\n        ).all()\n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\"booking_form.html\", {\n            \"request\": request,\n            \"user\": user,\n            \"instructor\": instructor,\n            \"existing_bookings\": existing_bookings,\n            \"error_message\": \"Invalid date or time format.\",\n            \"unread_count\": get_unread_count(db, user)\n        })\n    \n    # Conflict Check Logic\n    try:\n        new_start = datetime.strptime(f\"{date} {time}\", \"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M\")\n        new_end = new_start + timedelta(hours=duration)\n        \n        # Check against existing ACCEPTED bookings for this instructor\n        existing_accepted = db.query(models.BookingRequest).filter(\n            models.BookingRequest.instructor_id == instructor_id,\n            models.BookingRequest.status == \"accepted\",\n            models.BookingRequest.date == date\n        ).all()\n        \n        for booking in existing_accepted:\n            b_start = datetime.strptime(f\"{booking.date} {booking.time}\", \"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M\")\n            b_end = b_start + timedelta(hours=booking.duration)\n            \n            # Check for overlap: (StartA < EndB) and (EndA > StartB)\n            if new_start < b_end and new_end > b_start:\n                 # Fetch instructor and existing bookings again for re-rendering the form\n                instructor = db.query(models.InstructorProfile).filter(models.InstructorProfile.id == instructor_id).first()\n                existing_bookings = db.query(models.BookingRequest).filter(\n                    models.BookingRequest.student_id == user.id,\n                    models.BookingRequest.instructor_id == instructor_id,\n                    models.BookingRequest.status.in_([\"pending\", \"accepted\"])\n                ).all()\n                \n                return templates.TemplateResponse(\"booking_form.html\", {\n                    \"request\": request,\n                    \"user\": user,\n                    \"instructor\": instructor,\n                    \"existing_bookings\": existing_bookings,\n                    \"error_message\": \"The instructor is not available at the selected time. Please choose a different slot.\",\n                    \"unread_count\": get_unread_count(db, user)\n                })\n\n    except ValueError:\n        pass # Handle parsing errors if necessary\n\n    booking = models.BookingRequest(\n        student_id=user.id,\n        instructor_id=instructor_id,\n        date=date,\n        time=time,\n        duration=duration,\n        pickup_address=pickup_address,\n        notes=notes\n    )\n    db.add(booking)\n    db.commit()\n    \n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\"booking_success.html\", {\n        \"request\": request,\n        \"user\": user,\n        \"unread_count\": get_unread_count(db, user)\n    })\n\n@app.post(\"/booking/{booking_id}/{action}\")\nasync def handle_booking(\n    request: Request,\n    booking_id: int,\n    action: str,\n    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n    user: models.User = Depends(get_current_user)\n):\n    if not user or user.role != \"instructor\": return RedirectResponse(url=\"/login\")\n    \n    booking = db.query(models.BookingRequest).filter(models.BookingRequest.id == booking_id).first()\n    \n    # Ensure this booking belongs to the logged-in instructor\n    if not booking or booking.instructor.user_id != user.id:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=\"Not authorized\")\n        \n    if action == \"accept\":\n        booking.status = \"accepted\"\n    elif action == \"reject\":\n        booking.status = \"rejected\"\n        \n    db.commit()\n    return RedirectResponse(url=\"/dashboard\", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)\n\n@app.get(\"/log-session/{booking_id}\", response_class=HTMLResponse)\nasync def log_session_form(\n    request: Request, \n    booking_id: int, \n    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n    user: models.User = Depends(get_current_user)\n):\n    if not user or user.role != \"instructor\": return RedirectResponse(url=\"/login\")\n    \n    booking = db.query(models.BookingRequest).filter(models.BookingRequest.id == booking_id).first()\n    if not booking or booking.instructor.user_id != user.id:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=\"Not authorized\")\n    \n    if booking.status == \"completed\":\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=\"Session already completed. Cannot log again.\")\n        \n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\"log_session_form.html\", {\n        \"request\": request,\n        \"user\": user,\n        \"booking\": booking,\n        \"fault_map\": FAULT_MAP,\n        \"unread_count\": get_unread_count(db, user)\n    })\n\n@app.post(\"/log-session/{booking_id}\")\nasync def submit_session_log(\n    request: Request,\n    booking_id: int,\n    duration: int = Form(...),\n    weather: str = Form(...),\n    road_type: str = Form(...),\n    shared_feedback: str = Form(...),\n    private_notes: str = Form(\"\"),\n    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n    user: models.User = Depends(get_current_user)\n):\n    if not user or user.role != \"instructor\": return RedirectResponse(url=\"/login\")\n    \n    booking = db.query(models.BookingRequest).filter(models.BookingRequest.id == booking_id).first()\n    if not booking or booking.instructor.user_id != user.id:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=\"Not authorized\")\n        \n    if booking.status == \"completed\":\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=\"Session already completed. Cannot log again.\")\n    \n    # Process dynamic fault counts\n    form_data = await request.form()\n    \n    obs_list = []\n    space_list = []\n    speed_list = []\n    steering_list = []\n    communication_list = []\n    \n    for key, value in form_data.items():\n        if key.startswith(\"counts_\"):\n            code = key.split(\"_\")[1] # Extract code e.g. \"A1\"\n            try:\n                count = int(value)\n            except ValueError:\n                count = 0\n            \n            if count > 0:\n                # Add code 'count' times to the list\n                faults = [code] * count\n                \n                if code.startswith(\"A\"):\n                    obs_list.extend(faults)\n                elif code.startswith(\"B\"):\n                    space_list.extend(faults)\n                elif code.startswith(\"C\"):\n                    speed_list.extend(faults)\n                elif code.startswith(\"D\"):\n                    steering_list.extend(faults)\n                elif code.startswith(\"E\"):\n                    communication_list.extend(faults)\n\n    session = models.DrivingSession(\n        booking_id=booking_id,\n        duration_minutes=duration,\n        weather_condition=weather,\n        road_type=road_type,\n        observation_data=\",\".join(obs_list),\n        space_margin_data=\",\".join(space_list),\n        speed_data=\",\".join(speed_list),\n        steering_data=\",\".join(steering_list),\n        communication_data=\",\".join(communication_list),\n        shared_feedback=shared_feedback,\n        instructor_private_notes=private_notes,\n        created_at=datetime.now().strftime(\"%Y-%m-%d\")\n    )\n    \n    db.add(session)\n    \n    # Mark booking as completed\n    booking = db.query(models.BookingRequest).filter(models.BookingRequest.id == booking_id).first()\n    if booking:\n        booking.status = \"completed\"\n        \n    db.commit()\n    return RedirectResponse(url=\"/dashboard\", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)\n\n@app.get(\"/progress\", response_class=HTMLResponse)\nasync def student_progress(\n    request: Request,\n    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n    user: models.User = Depends(get_current_user)\n):\n    if not user or user.role != \"student\": return RedirectResponse(url=\"/login\")\n    \n    # Fetch all bookings for history (Merged view)\n    bookings = db.query(models.BookingRequest).filter(models.BookingRequest.student_id == user.id)\\\n        .order_by(models.BookingRequest.date.desc(), models.BookingRequest.time.desc()).all()\n\n    # Fetch sessions for stats (Keep existing logic or derive from bookings)\n    # Existing logic joins BookingRequest. Let's keep it to ensure we only count legitimate sessions.\n    sessions = db.query(models.DrivingSession).join(models.BookingRequest).filter(\n        models.BookingRequest.student_id == user.id\n    ).all()\n    \n    # Calculate Stats\n    total_lessons = len(sessions)\n    total_minutes = sum(s.duration_minutes for s in sessions)\n    total_hours = round(total_minutes / 60, 1)\n    \n    # Calculate Costs & Total Spent\n    total_spent = 0.0\n    for b in bookings:\n        rate = b.instructor.hourly_rate\n        b.cost = b.duration * rate\n        if b.status == 'completed':\n            total_spent += b.cost\n            \n    total_spent = round(total_spent, 2)\n    \n    # Aggregate Faults/Criteria Data\n    category_map = {\n        \"A\": \"Observation\",\n        \"B\": \"Space Margin\",\n        \"C\": \"Speed\",\n        \"D\": \"Steering\",\n        \"E\": \"Communication\"\n    }\n    \n    grouped_counts = {name: {} for name in category_map.values()}\n    \n    for s in sessions:\n        all_data = f\"{s.observation_data},{s.space_margin_data},{s.speed_data},{s.steering_data},{s.communication_data}\"\n        codes = [c.strip() for c in all_data.split(',') if c.strip()]\n        \n        for code in codes:\n            prefix = code[0].upper()\n            if prefix in category_map:\n                cat_name = category_map[prefix]\n                grouped_counts[cat_name][code] = grouped_counts[cat_name].get(code, 0) + 1\n\n    grouped_faults = {}\n    for cat_name, counts in grouped_counts.items():\n        sorted_items = sorted(counts.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        formatted_list = []\n        for code, count in sorted_items:\n            desc = FAULT_MAP.get(code, \"Unknown\")\n            formatted_list.append((code, desc, count))\n        if formatted_list:\n            grouped_faults[cat_name] = formatted_list\n    \n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\"student_progress.html\", {\n        \"request\": request,\n        \"user\": user,\n        \"bookings\": bookings, # Full history\n        \"stats\": {\n            \"total_lessons\": total_lessons,\n            \"total_hours\": total_hours,\n            \"total_spent\": total_spent\n        },\n        \"grouped_faults\": grouped_faults,\n        \"unread_count\": get_unread_count(db, user)\n    })\n\n@app.get(\"/instructor-progress\", response_class=HTMLResponse)\nasync def instructor_progress(\n    request: Request,\n    student_id: Optional[int] = None,\n    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n    user: models.User = Depends(get_current_user)\n):\n    if not user or user.role != \"instructor\": return RedirectResponse(url=\"/login\")\n    \n    # 1. Fetch Students (for the filter list)\n    students = db.query(models.User).join(models.BookingRequest, models.BookingRequest.student_id == models.User.id)\\\n        .filter(models.BookingRequest.instructor_id == user.instructor_profile.id)\\\n        .distinct().all()\n\n    # 2. Fetch Sessions (for Stats & Faults)\n    session_query = db.query(models.DrivingSession).join(models.BookingRequest).filter(\n        models.BookingRequest.instructor_id == user.instructor_profile.id\n    )\n    if student_id:\n        session_query = session_query.filter(models.BookingRequest.student_id == student_id)\n    sessions = session_query.all()\n\n    # 3. Fetch Bookings (for History List)\n    booking_query = db.query(models.BookingRequest).filter(\n        models.BookingRequest.instructor_id == user.instructor_profile.id\n    ).order_by(models.BookingRequest.date.desc(), models.BookingRequest.time.desc())\n    \n    if student_id:\n        booking_query = booking_query.filter(models.BookingRequest.student_id == student_id)\n    bookings = booking_query.all()\n    \n    # 4. Calculate Earnings\n    hourly_rate = user.instructor_profile.hourly_rate\n    total_earnings = 0.0\n    \n    for b in bookings:\n        if b.status == 'completed':\n            b.earnings = round(b.duration * hourly_rate, 2)\n            total_earnings += b.earnings\n        else:\n            b.earnings = 0.0\n            \n    total_earnings = round(total_earnings, 2)\n    \n    # 5. Calculate Stats\n    total_lessons = len(sessions)\n    total_minutes = sum(s.duration_minutes for s in sessions)\n    total_hours = round(total_minutes / 60, 1)\n    \n    # 6. Aggregate Faults by Category\n    category_map = {\n        \"A\": \"Observation\",\n        \"B\": \"Space Margin\",\n        \"C\": \"Speed\",\n        \"D\": \"Steering\",\n        \"E\": \"Communication\"\n    }\n    \n    grouped_counts = {name: {} for name in category_map.values()}\n    \n    for s in sessions:\n        all_data = f\"{s.observation_data},{s.space_margin_data},{s.speed_data},{s.steering_data},{s.communication_data}\"\n        codes = [c.strip() for c in all_data.split(',') if c.strip()]\n        \n        for code in codes:\n            prefix = code[0].upper()\n            if prefix in category_map:\n                cat_name = category_map[prefix]\n                grouped_counts[cat_name][code] = grouped_counts[cat_name].get(code, 0) + 1\n\n    grouped_faults = {}\n    for cat_name, counts in grouped_counts.items():\n        sorted_items = sorted(counts.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        formatted_list = []\n        for code, count in sorted_items:\n            desc = FAULT_MAP.get(code, \"Unknown\")\n            formatted_list.append((code, desc, count))\n        if formatted_list:\n            grouped_faults[cat_name] = formatted_list\n    \n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\"instructor_progress.html\", {\n        \"request\": request,\n        \"user\": user,\n        \"bookings\": bookings,\n        \"students\": students,\n        \"selected_student_id\": student_id,\n        \"total_earnings\": total_earnings,\n        \"stats\": {\n            \"total_lessons\": total_lessons,\n            \"total_hours\": total_hours\n        },\n        \"grouped_faults\": grouped_faults,\n        \"unread_count\": get_unread_count(db, user)\n    })\n\n# --- Cancellation Routes ---\n\n@app.post(\"/cancel-booking/{booking_id}\")\nasync def cancel_booking(\n    request: Request,\n    booking_id: int,\n    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n    user: models.User = Depends(get_current_user)\n):\n    if not user: return RedirectResponse(url=\"/login\")\n    \n    booking = db.query(models.BookingRequest).filter(models.BookingRequest.id == booking_id).first()\n    if not booking or booking.student_id != user.id:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=\"Not authorized\")\n        \n    if booking.status == \"completed\":\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=\"Cannot cancel a completed session.\")\n        \n    # Check 24h rule\n    try:\n        booking_dt = datetime.strptime(f\"{booking.date} {booking.time}\", \"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M\")\n        time_diff = booking_dt - datetime.now()\n        \n        # Simple penalty logic (in real app, charge user)\n        if time_diff < timedelta(hours=24):\n            print(f\"PENALTY: Student {user.email} cancelled booking {booking_id} within 24h. 25% fee applies.\")\n            \n        booking.status = \"cancelled\"\n        db.commit()\n        \n    except ValueError:\n        pass \n        \n    return RedirectResponse(url=\"/dashboard\", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)\n\n@app.post(\"/request-cancellation/{booking_id}\")\nasync def request_cancellation(\n    request: Request,\n    booking_id: int,\n    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n    user: models.User = Depends(get_current_user)\n):\n    if not user or user.role != \"instructor\": return RedirectResponse(url=\"/login\")\n    \n    booking = db.query(models.BookingRequest).filter(models.BookingRequest.id == booking_id).first()\n    if not booking or booking.instructor.user_id != user.id:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=\"Not authorized\")\n    \n    if booking.status == \"completed\":\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=\"Cannot request cancellation for a completed session.\")\n        \n    if booking.status == \"accepted\":\n        booking.status = \"cancellation_requested\"\n        db.commit()\n        \n    return RedirectResponse(url=\"/dashboard\", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)\n\n@app.post(\"/respond-cancellation/{booking_id}/{action}\")\nasync def respond_cancellation(\n    request: Request,\n    booking_id: int,\n    action: str,\n    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n    user: models.User = Depends(get_current_user)\n):\n    if not user: return RedirectResponse(url=\"/login\") # Student responding\n    \n    booking = db.query(models.BookingRequest).filter(models.BookingRequest.id == booking_id).first()\n    if not booking or booking.student_id != user.id:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=\"Not authorized\")\n        \n    if action == \"approve\":\n        booking.status = \"cancelled\"\n    elif action == \"deny\":\n        booking.status = \"accepted\"\n        \n    db.commit()\n    return RedirectResponse(url=\"/dashboard\", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)\n\n# --- Messaging Routes ---\n\n@app.get(\"/messages\", response_class=HTMLResponse)\nasync def view_messages(\n    request: Request,\n    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n    user: models.User = Depends(get_current_user)\n):\n    if not user: return RedirectResponse(url=\"/login\")\n    \n    # Find all unique users communicated with\n    # (Sent to OR Received from)\n    from sqlalchemy import or_\n    \n    # IDs of people I sent to\n    sent_ids = db.query(models.Message.recipient_id).filter(models.Message.sender_id == user.id)\n    # IDs of people I received from\n    received_ids = db.query(models.Message.sender_id).filter(models.Message.recipient_id == user.id)\n    \n    contact_ids = sent_ids.union(received_ids).all()\n    contact_ids = [i[0] for i in contact_ids]\n    \n    contacts = db.query(models.User).filter(models.User.id.in_(contact_ids)).all()\n    \n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\"messages_list.html\", {\n        \"request\": request,\n        \"user\": user,\n        \"contacts\": contacts,\n        \"unread_count\": get_unread_count(db, user)\n    })\n\n@app.get(\"/messages/{other_user_id}\", response_class=HTMLResponse)\nasync def chat_view(\n    request: Request,\n    other_user_id: int,\n    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n    user: models.User = Depends(get_current_user)\n):\n    if not user: return RedirectResponse(url=\"/login\")\n    \n    other_user = db.query(models.User).filter(models.User.id == other_user_id).first()\n    if not other_user:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=\"User not found\")\n        \n    # Fetch conversation\n    from sqlalchemy import or_, and_\n    messages = db.query(models.Message).filter(\n        or_(\n            and_(models.Message.sender_id == user.id, models.Message.recipient_id == other_user_id),\n            and_(models.Message.sender_id == other_user_id, models.Message.recipient_id == user.id)\n        )\n    ).order_by(models.Message.timestamp.asc()).all()\n    \n    # Mark as read\n    for m in messages:\n        if m.recipient_id == user.id and not m.is_read:\n            m.is_read = True\n    db.commit()\n    \n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\"chat.html\", {\n        \"request\": request,\n        \"user\": user,\n        \"other_user\": other_user,\n        \"messages\": messages,\n        \"unread_count\": get_unread_count(db, user)\n    })\n\n@app.post(\"/send-message\")\nasync def send_message(\n    request: Request,\n    recipient_id: int = Form(...),\n    content: str = Form(...),\n    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n    user: models.User = Depends(get_current_user)\n):\n    if not user: return RedirectResponse(url=\"/login\")\n    \n    msg = models.Message(\n        sender_id=user.id,\n        recipient_id=recipient_id,\n        content=content,\n        timestamp=datetime.now().isoformat()\n    )\n    db.add(msg)\n    db.commit()\n    \n    return RedirectResponse(url=f\"/messages/{recipient_id}\", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)\n"
  }

```json
    "old_string": "    if user and user.instructor_profile:\n        user.instructor_profile.is_available = not user.instructor_profile.is_available\n        db.commit()\n    return RedirectResponse(url=\"/dashboard\", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)\n\n@app.get(\"/book/{instructor_id}\", response_class=HTMLResponse)",
    "new_string": "    if user and user.instructor_profile:\n        user.instructor_profile.is_available = not user.instructor_profile.is_available\n        db.commit()\n    return RedirectResponse(url=\"/dashboard\", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)\n\n@app.post(\"/availability/add\")\nasync def add_availability(\n    request: Request,\n    day_of_week: int = Form(...),\n    start_time: str = Form(...),\n    end_time: str = Form(...),\n    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n    user: models.User = Depends(get_current_user)\n):\n    if not user or user.role != \"instructor\": return RedirectResponse(url=\"/login\")\n    \n    # Simple validation\n    if start_time >= end_time:\n        # In a real app, we'd flash an error\n        return RedirectResponse(url=\"/dashboard\", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)\n\n    availability = models.InstructorAvailability(\n        instructor_id=user.instructor_profile.id,\n        day_of_week=day_of_week,\n        start_time=start_time,\n        end_time=end_time\n    )\n    db.add(availability)\n    db.commit()\n    return RedirectResponse(url=\"/dashboard\", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)\n\n@app.post(\"/availability/delete/{availability_id}\")\nasync def delete_availability(\n    request: Request,\n    availability_id: int,\n    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n    user: models.User = Depends(get_current_user)\n):\n    if not user or user.role != \"instructor\": return RedirectResponse(url=\"/login\")\n    \n    avail = db.query(models.InstructorAvailability).filter(\n        models.InstructorAvailability.id == availability_id,\n        models.InstructorAvailability.instructor_id == user.instructor_profile.id\n    ).first()\n    \n    if avail:\n        db.delete(avail)\n        db.commit()\n        \n    return RedirectResponse(url=\"/dashboard\", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)\n\n@app.get(\"/book/{instructor_id}\", response_class=HTMLResponse)",
    "file_path": "/home/Hoss/Documents/road-ready/app/main.py"
```

```json
{
```

```json
    "content": "\nimport sys\nimport os\nfrom datetime import datetime, timedelta\n\n# Add local lib directory to sys.path\nsys.path.append(os.path.join(os.getcwd(), \"lib\"))\n\nfrom app import models, database\nfrom sqlalchemy.orm import Session\n\ndef test_availability_logic():\n    db = database.SessionLocal()\n    try:\n        # 1. Create a dummy instructor\n        user = models.User(email=\"inst_test@example.com\", full_name=\"Inst Test\", role=\"instructor\")\n        db.add(user)\n        db.commit()\n        \n        profile = models.InstructorProfile(user_id=user.id, city=\"Test City\", hourly_rate=50)\n        db.add(profile)\n        db.commit()\n        \n        print(f\"Created Instructor ID: {profile.id}\")\n        \n        # 2. Add Availability: Monday (0) 09:00 - 12:00\n        avail = models.InstructorAvailability(\n            instructor_id=profile.id,\n            day_of_week=0,\n            start_time=\"09:00\",\n            end_time=\"12:00\"\n        )\n        db.add(avail)\n        db.commit()\n        print(\"Added Availability: Mon 09:00-12:00\")\n        \n        # 3. Test Booking Valid (Mon 10:00 - 11:00)\n        # We need a date that is a Monday. \n        # Jan 1, 2024 was a Monday.\n        test_date = \"2024-01-01\" \n        test_time = \"10:00\"\n        duration = 1\n        \n        # Logic from main.py\n        new_start = datetime.strptime(f\"{test_date} {test_time}\", \"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M\")\n        new_end = new_start + timedelta(hours=duration)\n        \n        day_of_week = new_start.weekday() # Should be 0\n        is_valid_slot = False\n        \n        for slot in profile.availabilities:\n            if slot.day_of_week == day_of_week:\n                slot_start = datetime.strptime(f\"{test_date} {slot.start_time}\", \"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M\")\n                slot_end = datetime.strptime(f\"{test_date} {slot.end_time}\", \"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M\")\n                \n                if new_start >= slot_start and new_end <= slot_end:\n                    is_valid_slot = True\n                    break\n                    \n        print(f\"Booking Mon 10-11 Valid? {is_valid_slot} (Expected: True)\")\n        \n        # 4. Test Booking Invalid (Mon 13:00 - 14:00)\n        test_time_invalid = \"13:00\"\n        new_start_inv = datetime.strptime(f\"{test_date} {test_time_invalid}\", \"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M\")\n        new_end_inv = new_start_inv + timedelta(hours=duration)\n        \n        is_valid_slot_inv = False\n        for slot in profile.availabilities:\n             if slot.day_of_week == day_of_week:\n                slot_start = datetime.strptime(f\"{test_date} {slot.start_time}\", \"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M\")\n                slot_end = datetime.strptime(f\"{test_date} {slot.end_time}\", \"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M\")\n                \n                if new_start_inv >= slot_start and new_end_inv <= slot_end:\n                    is_valid_slot_inv = True\n                    break\n        \n        print(f\"Booking Mon 13-14 Valid? {is_valid_slot_inv} (Expected: False)\")\n\n        # Cleanup\n        db.delete(avail)\n        db.delete(profile)\n        db.delete(user)\n        db.commit()\n        \n    finally:\n        db.close()\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    test_availability_logic()\n"
  }
}
```

## MODEL ✨

I have implemented the **Availability Calendar** feature.

**Changes made:**

1.  **Database & Models:**
    *   Created a new `InstructorAvailability` table to store working hours (Day of Week, Start Time, End Time).
    *   Linked it to the `InstructorProfile`.

2.  **Instructor Dashboard:**
    *   Added a **"Working Hours"** section.
    *   Instructors can now add weekly recurring slots (e.g., "Mondays 09:00 - 17:00").
    *   Instructors can remove existing slots.

3.  **Booking Logic:**
    *   Updated the booking process to automatically validate requests against the instructor's set availability.
    *   If a student tries to book outside of these hours, they will receive a specific error message advising them to check the working hours.
    *   *Note: If an instructor has NO availability set, the system defaults to allowing bookings at any time (to preserve backward compatibility).*

You can verify this by logging in as an instructor, setting your hours, and then trying to book a lesson as a student outside of those hours.

Would you like to proceed with the next feature, **Progress Analytics**, or refine this one further?

style=\"font-size: 0.7em;\">{{ count }}</span>\n                            </li>\n                            {% endfor %}\n                        </ul>\n                    {% endfor %}\n if (!ctx) return;\n\n        // Data from Backend\n        const labels = {{ category_totals.keys() | list | tojson }};\n        const data = {{ category_totals.values() | list | tojson }};\n\n Chart(ctx, {\n            type: 'bar',\n            data: {\n                labels: labels,\n                datasets: [{\n                    label: 'Faults Recorded',\n                    data: ## MODEL ✨

I have implemented the **Progress Analytics** feature.

**Changes made:**

1.  **Backend (`app/main.py`):**
    *   Calculated `category_totals` (sum of faults per category) in the `student_progress` route.
    *   Passed this data to the template.

2.  **Frontend (`app/templates/student_progress.html`):**
    *   Added **Chart.js** via CDN.
    *   Replaced the top of the "Areas for Improvement" section with a visual **Bar Chart** showing fault distribution.
    *   If no data is available (new student), it displays a friendly message.

**How to verify:**
1.  Navigate to [http://localhost:8000](http://localhost:8000).
2.  Login as a Student (who has completed lessons with logged faults).
3.  Click on **"My Progress"**.
4.  You should see a Bar Chart visualizing your fault counts by category (Observation, Speed, etc.).

## MODEL ✨

I will implement **Practice Quizzes** to help students prepare for their theory tests.

Here is the plan:
1.  **Database:** Add a `QuizQuestion` model to store questions, options, and answers.
2.  **Seed Data:** Create a script to populate the database with some sample driving theory questions (based on BC rules).
3.  **Backend:** Add routes to fetch a quiz (random set of questions) and submit answers.
4.  **Frontend:** Create a `quiz.html` template to take the test and see results.

## MODEL ✨

I will now create a new seed script `seed_quiz.py` to populate the database with some sample questions.**Tool Command**:
```json
{
  "name": "write_file",
  "args": {
    "file_path": "seed_quiz.py",
    "content": "\nimport sys\nimport os\n\n# Add local lib directory to sys.path\nsys.path.append(os.path.join(os.getcwd(), \"lib\"))\n\nfrom app import models, database\nfrom sqlalchemy.orm import Session\n\ndef seed_quiz_data():\n    db = database.SessionLocal()\n    \n    # Check if questions already exist\n    if db.query(models.QuizQuestion).count() > 0:\n        print(\"Quiz questions already seeded.\")\n        return\n\n    questions = [\n        {\n            \"question_text\": \"When a school bus displays flashing red lights and the stop arm is extended, you must:\",\n            \"option_a\": \"Slow down and pass with caution.\",\n            \"option_b\": \"Stop at least 5 meters away.\",\n            \"option_c\": \"Stop only if you are behind the bus.\",\n            \"option_d\": \"Honk to warn children.\",\n            \"correct_option\": \"B\",\n            \"explanation\": \"You must stop whether you are approaching from the front or the rear (unless on a divided highway).\"\n        },\n        {\n            \"question_text\": \"What does a flashing green traffic light mean?\",\n            \"option_a\": \"The light is about to turn red.\",\n            \"option_b\": \"It is a pedestrian controlled light.\",\n            \"option_c\": \"You have the right of way to turn left.\",\n            \"option_d\": \"Stop and proceed when safe.\",\n            \"correct_option\": \"B\",\n            \"explanation\": \"A flashing green light indicates a pedestrian-controlled light. Proceed with caution if the intersection is clear.\"\n        },\n        {\n            \"question_text\": \"When are you allowed to make a U-turn?\",\n            \"option_a\": \"On a curve where you can be seen by other drivers.\",\n            \"option_b\": \"At any intersection with a traffic light.\",\n            \"option_c\": \"If there is no sign prohibiting it and you can do so safely without interfering with traffic.\",\n            \"option_d\": \"On the crest of a hill.\",\n            \"correct_option\": \"C\",\n            \"explanation\": \"U-turns are generally permitted if safe and not prohibited by a sign. Never do a U-turn on a curve or hill.\"\n        },\n        {\n            \"question_text\": \"The 2-second rule is used to determine:\",\n            \"option_a\": \"How long to wait at a stop sign.\",\n            \"option_b\": \"A safe following distance in good weather.\",\n            \"option_c\": \"How fast to accelerate.\",\n            \"option_d\": \"The time it takes to check your mirrors.\",\n            \"correct_option\": \"B\",\n            \"explanation\": \"Keep at least 2 seconds of distance between you and the vehicle ahead in ideal conditions.\"\n        },\n        {\n            \"question_text\": \"If your car starts to skid, what should you do?\",\n            \"option_a\": \"Brake hard immediately.\",\n            \"option_b\": \"Accelerate to gain traction.\",\n            \"option_c\": \"Steer in the direction you want the front of the vehicle to go.\",\n            \"option_d\": \"Let go of the steering wheel.\",\n            \"correct_option\": \"C\",\n            \"explanation\": \"Ease off the gas and steer in the direction you want to go. Do not slam on the brakes.\"\n        }\n    ]\n\n    for q in questions:\n        question = models.QuizQuestion(**q)\n        db.add(question)\n    \n    db.commit()\n    print(f\"Seeded {len(questions)} quiz questions.\")\n    db.close()\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    # Ensure tables exist (including the new one)\n    models.Base.metadata.create_all(bind=database.engine)\n    seed_quiz_data()\n"
  }
}
```

## MODEL ✨

I will now add the `quiz` route to `app/main.py`. This route will fetch 5 random questions and display them. I'll also add a `submit_quiz` route to calculate the score.**Tool Command**:
```json
{
  "name": "replace",
  "args": {
    "file_path": "/home/Hoss/Documents/road-ready/app/main.py",
    "new_string": "@app.get(\"/education/bc\", response_class=HTMLResponse)\nasync def education_book_bc(request: Request, user: models.User = Depends(get_current_user), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\"education_book.html\", {\n        \"request\": request,\n        \"user\": user,\n        \"unread_count\": get_unread_count(db, user) if user else 0\n    })\n\n@app.get(\"/education/quiz\", response_class=HTMLResponse)\nasync def education_quiz(request: Request, user: models.User = Depends(get_current_user), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):\n    # Fetch 5 random questions\n    from sqlalchemy.sql.expression import func\n    questions = db.query(models.QuizQuestion).order_by(func.random()).limit(5).all()\n    \n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\"quiz.html\", {\n        \"request\": request,\n        \"user\": user,\n        \"questions\": questions,\n        \"unread_count\": get_unread_count(db, user) if user else 0\n    })\n\n@app.post(\"/education/quiz/submit\", response_class=HTMLResponse)\nasync def submit_quiz(\n    request: Request,\n    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n    user: models.User = Depends(get_current_user)\n):\n    form_data = await request.form()\n    \n    score = 0\n    results = []\n    \n    # Process each submitted answer\n    for key, value in form_data.items():\n        if key.startswith(\"q_\"):\n            q_id = int(key.split(\"_\")[1])\n            selected_option = value\n            \n            question = db.query(models.QuizQuestion).filter(models.QuizQuestion.id == q_id).first()\n            if question:\n                is_correct = (selected_option == question.correct_option)\n                if is_correct:\n                    score += 1\n                \n                results.append({\n                    \"question\": question,\n                    \"selected\": selected_option,\n                    \"is_correct\": is_correct\n                })\n    \n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\"quiz_result.html\", {\n        \"request\": request,\n        \"user\": user,\n        \"score\": score,\n        \"total\": len(results),\n        \"results\": results,\n        \"unread_count\": get_unread_count(db, user) if user else 0\n    })\n\n@app.get(\"/\", response_class=HTMLResponse)",
    "old_string": "@app.get(\"/education/bc\", response_class=HTMLResponse)\nasync def education_book_bc(request: Request, user: models.User = Depends(get_current_user), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\"education_book.html\", {\n        \"request\": request,\n        \"user\": user,\n        \"unread_count\": get_unread_count(db, user) if user else 0\n    })\n\n@app.get(\"/\", response_class=HTMLResponse)",
    "instruction": "Add /education/quiz and /education/quiz/submit routes."
  }
}
```

```json
  }
```

## MODEL ✨

I have implemented the **Practice Quizzes** feature.

**Changes made:**

1.  **Database:** Added `QuizQuestion` model.
2.  **Seed Data:** Created and ran `seed_quiz.py` to add 5 sample BC driving theory questions.
3.  **Backend (`app/main.py`):**
    *   Added `/education/quiz` route to fetch random questions.
    *   Added `/education/quiz/submit` route to grade the submission.
4.  **Frontend:**
    *   Created `quiz.html` for taking the test.
    *   Created `quiz_result.html` for viewing scores and explanations.
    *   Updated `education_home.html` to link to the quiz section.

**How to verify:**
1.  Navigate to [http://localhost:8000/education](http://localhost:8000/education) (or click "Education" in the nav bar).
2.  Click the new **"Start Quiz"** card.
3.  Answer the questions and click "Submit".
4.  View your score and read the explanations for any wrong answers.

```json
  "args": {
```
```json
    "content": "\nimport sys\nimport os\n\n# Add local lib directory to sys.path\nsys.path.append(os.path.join(os.getcwd(), \"lib\"))\n\nfrom app import models, database\nfrom sqlalchemy.orm import Session\n\ndef seed_large_quiz_data():\n    db = database.SessionLocal()\n    \n    # Clear existing questions to avoid duplicates/confusion\n    db.query(models.QuizQuestion).delete()\n    db.commit()\n\n    questions = [\n        # --- SIGNS & SIGNALS ---\n        {\n            \"question_text\": \"What does a flashing red traffic light mean?\",\n            \"option_a\": \"Stop, then proceed when safe (like a Stop sign).\",\n            \"option_b\": \"Slow down and proceed with caution.\",\n            \"option_c\": \"The traffic light is broken.\",\n            \"option_d\": \"Stop and wait for the green light.\",\n            \"correct_option\": \"A\",\n            \"explanation\": \"A flashing red light acts exactly like a stop sign.\"\n        },\n        {\n            \"question_text\": \"A yellow diamond-shaped sign with a black cross indicates:\",\n            \"option_a\": \"Hospital ahead.\",\n            \"option_b\": \"Intersection ahead.\",\n            \"option_c\": \"Railway crossing.\",\n            \"option_d\": \"First aid station.\",\n            \"correct_option\": \"B\",\n            \"explanation\": \"Yellow diamond signs are warning signs. A cross indicates an intersection.\"\n        },\n        {\n            \"question_text\": \"What does a steady yellow traffic light mean?\",\n            \"option_a\": \"Speed up to clear the intersection.\",\n            \"option_b\": \"Stop if you can do so safely; otherwise, proceed with caution.\",\n            \"option_c\": \"Stop immediately, regardless of position.\",\n            \"option_d\": \"The light will turn red in 5 seconds.\",\n            \"correct_option\": \"B\",\n            \"explanation\": \"You must stop if it is safe. If you are too close to stop safely, continue through.\"\n        },\n        {\n            \"question_text\": \"A round green sign with a white circle around a bicycle means:\",\n            \"option_a\": \"Bicycles are prohibited.\",\n            \"option_b\": \"Bicycle crossing ahead.\",\n            \"option_c\": \"Bicycle lane - bicycles only.\",\n            \"option_d\": \"Share the road with bicycles.\",\n            \"correct_option\": \"C\",\n            \"explanation\": \"A green circle usually indicates a permissive regulation, in this case, a lane reserved for cyclists.\"\n        },\n        {\n            \"question_text\": \"What does a 'Merge' sign look like?\",\n            \"option_a\": \"Yellow diamond with two lines converging.\",\n            \"option_b\": \"Red octagon.\",\n            \"option_c\": \"White rectangle.\",\n            \"option_d\": \"Orange triangle.\",\n            \"correct_option\": \"A\",\n            \"explanation\": \"Merge signs are warning signs (yellow diamond) showing two paths becoming one.\"\n        },\n        \n        # Pages: ' + pdf.numPages);\n            \n            // Generate DOM elements for pages\n            // Rendering ALL pages to canvas upfront is heavy. \n            // However, StPageFlip needs DOM elements.\n            // Optimization: We render pages, but maybe with lower initial scale or rely on browser caching.\n            \n            const renderPromises = [];\n            \n            // Limit for prototype if pdf is huge, but user asked for \"full book\".\n            // We will loop all.\n            for (let i = 1; i <= pdf.numPages; i++) {\n                renderPromises.push(\n                    pdf.getPage(i).then(page => {\n                        // Create container\n                        const div = document.createElement('div');\n                        div.className = 'my-page';\n                        div.id = `page-${i}`;\n                        \n                        const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');\n                        div.appendChild(canvas);\n                        \n                        // Viewport\n                        // Scale 1.5 for decent quality on flip\n                        const viewport = page.getViewport({ scale: 1.5 });\n                        canvas.height = viewport.height;\n                        canvas.width = viewport.width;\n                        \n                        const renderContext = {\n                            canvasContext: canvas.getContext('2d'),\n                            viewport: viewport\n                        };\n                        \n                        return page.render(renderContext).promise.then(() => {\n                            return { dom: div, index: i };\n                        });\n                    })\n                );\n            }\n\n            // Wait for all to render (sequentially or parallel? Parallel might freeze UI)\n            // Let's Promise.all\n            try {\n                const results = await Promise.all(renderPromises);\n                \n                // Sort by index to ensure correct order\n                results.sort((a, b) => a.index - b.index);\n                \n                // Append to DOM\n                results.forEach(res => bookElem.appendChild(res.dom));\n                \n                // Hide loader, show book\n                loader.style.display = 'none';\n                bookElem.style.display = 'block';\n                controls.style.display = 'flex';\n                \n                // Init Flip\n                const pageFlip = new St.PageFlip(bookElem, {\n                    width: 550, // match css\n                    height: 780, // match css\n                    size: 'stretch',\n                    minWidth: 300,\n                    maxWidth: 1000,\n                    minHeight: 400,\n                    maxHeight: 1400,\n                    maxShadowOpacity: 0.5,\n                    showCover: true,\n                    mobileScrollSupport: false \n                });\n\n                pageFlip.loadFromHTML(document.querySelectorAll('.my-page'));\n                \n                // Event Listeners\n                document.getElementById('prevBtn').addEventListener('click', () => pageFlip.flipPrev());\n                document.getElementById('nextBtn').addEventListener('click', () => pageFlip.flipNext());\n                \n                pageFlip.on('flip', (e) => {\n                    pageInfo.textContent = `Page ${e.data + 1} of ${pageFlip.getPageCount()}`;\n                });\n                \n                pageInfo.textContent = `Page 1 of ${pageFlip.getPageCount()}`;\n\n            } catch (error) {\n                console.error('Error rendering pages:', error);\n                loader.innerHTML = '<p class=\"text-danger\">Error loading book. Please try again.</p>';\n            }\n\n        }).catch(err => {\n            console.error('Error loading PDF:', err);\n            loader.innerHTML = '<p class=\"text-danger\">Could not load the PDF file.</p>';\n        });\n    });\n</script>\n{% endblock %}\n"
```

## MODEL ✨

The `education_book.html` renders a PDF using a flipbook library. This makes deep-linking to specific "chapters" (HTML anchors) difficult because the content is inside a canvas/PDF, not semantic HTML text.

However, I can update the flipbook logic to support jumping to a specific page if a `?page=X` query parameter is present.

Here is the revised plan:

1.  **Define Chapter-to-Page Mapping:** I will map the chapters to their approximate page numbers in the `driver-full.pdf`. (I'll estimate based on standard structure or use generic starts).
    *   Chapter 1: Page 5
    *   Chapter 2: Page 15
    *   Chapter 3: Page 30
    *   Chapter 4: Page 50
    *   Chapter 5: Page 70
    *   Chapter 6: Page 90
    *   Chapter 7: Page 110
    *   Chapter 8: Page 120
    *   *Note: Since I can't see the PDF pages directly, these are estimates, but the mechanism will work.*

2.  **Frontend (`app/templates/education_book.html`):** Update the JavaScript to check for `urlParams` on load. If `page=X` exists, tell the `pageFlip` object to flip to that page after initialization.

3.  **Backend (`app/main.py`):** Create the `linkify_chapter` filter. It will replace `(See Chapter X...)` with `<a href="/education/bc?page=PAGE_NUM" target="_blank">`.

4.  **Frontend (`app/templates/quiz_result.html`):** Use the new filter.

I will start by updating `app/main.py` to add the custom filter.**Tool Command**:
```json
{
  "name": "replace",
  "args": {
    "instruction": "Add linkify_chapter filter to template environment.",
    "old_string": "    desc = FAULT_MAP.get(code, code)\n        expanded.append(f\"{code} - {desc}\")\n    return \", \".join(expanded)\n\ntemplates.env.filters[\"expand_codes\"] = expand_codes\n\n# Dependency\ndef get_db():",
    "file_path": "/home/Hoss/Documents/road-ready/app/main.py",
    "new_string": "    desc = FAULT_MAP.get(code, code)\n        expanded.append(f\"{code} - {desc}\")\n    return \", \".join(expanded)\n\ndef linkify_chapter(text: str) -> str:\n    if not text: return \"\"\n    import re\n    \n    # Map Chapters to estimated Page Numbers\n    # (In a real app, these would be precise based on the PDF)\n    chapter_map = {\n        \"1\": 5,\n        \"2\": 15,\n        \"3\": 30,\n        \"4\": 50,\n        \"5\": 70,\n        \"6\": 90,\n        \"7\": 110,\n        \"8\": 120\n    }\n    \n    def replace_match(match):\n        full_match = match.group(0) # e.g. (See Chapter 3: Signs...)\n        chapter_num = match.group(1)\n        \n        page = chapter_map.get(chapter_num, 1)\n        \n        # Strip the parens for the link text\n        link_text = full_match.strip(\"()\")\n        \n        return f'(<a href=\"/education/bc?page={page}\" target=\"_blank\" class=\"text-decoration-none\">{link_text} 🔗</a>)'\n\n    # Regex to find \"Chapter X\"\n    # Matches \"(See Chapter X\" or just \"Chapter X\"\n    pattern = r\"\\(See Chapter (\\d+).*?\\)\"\n    return re.sub(pattern, replace_match, text)\n\ntemplates.env.filters[\"expand_codes\"] = expand_codes\ntemplates.env.filters[\"linkify_chapter\"] = linkify_chapter\n\n# Dependency\ndef get_db():"
  }
}
```

</div>\n</div>\n\n<script>\n    let map, pickupMarker, dropoffMarker;\n    const routeModal = new bootstrap.Modal(document.getElementById('routeModal'));\n\n    function initMap() {\n        if (map) return;\n        // Default to Vancouver area\n        map = L.map('map').setView([49.2827, -123.1207], 11);\n        L.tileLayer('https://{s}.tile.openstreetmap.org/{z}/{x}/{y}.png', {\n            attribution: '© OpenStreetMap contributors'\n        }).addTo(map);\n\n        map.on('click', function(e) {\n            if (!pickupMarker) {\n                pickupMarker = L.marker(e.latlng, {draggable: true, icon: L.icon({\n                    iconUrl: 'https://raw.githubusercontent.com/pointhi/leaflet-color-markers/master/img/marker-icon-2x-green.png',\n                    shadowUrl: 'https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/leaflet/0.7.7/images/marker-shadow.png',\n                    iconSize: [25, 41], iconAnchor: [12, 41], popupAnchor: [1, -34], shadowSize: [41, 41]\n                })}).addTo(map).bindPopup(\"Pickup\").openPopup();\n                updateInputs();\n            } else if (!dropoffMarker) {\n                dropoffMarker = L.marker(e.latlng, {draggable: true, icon: L.icon({\n                    iconUrl: 'https://raw.githubusercontent.com/pointhi/leaflet-color-markers/master/img/marker-icon-2x-red.png',\n                    shadowUrl: 'https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/leaflet/0.7.7/images/marker-shadow.png',\n                    iconSize: [25, 41], iconAnchor: [12, 41], popupAnchor: [1, -34], shadowSize: [41, 41]\n                })}).addTo(map).bindPopup(\"Drop-off\").openPopup();\n                updateInputs();\n            }\n        });\n    }\n\n    function updateInputs() {\n        if (pickupMarker) {\n            document.getElementById('p_lat').value = pickupMarker.getLatLng().lat;\n            document.getElementById('p_lng').value = pickupMarker.getLatLng().lng;\n        }\n        if (dropoffMarker) {\n            document.getElementById('d_lat').value = dropoffMarker.getLatLng().lat;\n            document.getElementById('d_lng').value = dropoffMarker.getLatLng().lng;\n        }\n    }\n\n    function resetMarkers() {\n        if (pickupMarker) map.removeLayer(pickupMarker);\n        if (dropoffMarker) map.removeLayer(dropoffMarker);\n        pickupMarker = null;\n        dropoffMarker = null;\n        document.getElementById('p_lat').value = '';\n        document.getElementById('p_lng').value = '';\n        document.getElementById('d_lat').value = '';\n        document.getElementById('d_lng').value = '';\n    }\n\n    function openRouteModal(id, name, pLat, pLng, dLat, dLng) {\n        document.getElementById('modalStudentName').textContent = name;\n        document.getElementById('routeForm').action = `/booking/${id}/route`;\n        \n        routeModal.show();\n        \n        // Timeout to fix Leaflet gray tiles in modal\n        setTimeout(() => {\n            initMap();\n            map.invalidateSize();\n            resetMarkers();\n\n            if (pLat && pLng) {\n                pickupMarker = L.marker([pLat, pLng], {draggable: true}).addTo(map).bindPopup(\"Pickup\");\n                map.setView([pLat, pLng], 13);\n            }\n            if (dLat && dLng) {\n                dropoffMarker = L.marker([dLat, dLng], {draggable: true}).addTo(map).bindPopup(\"Drop-off\");\n            }\n            updateInputs();\n        }, 300);\n    }\n</script>\n{% endblock %}",

I will now add the JavaScript logic to `app/templates/student_dashboard.html` to handle displaying and initializing the map for students when they click the "View Route" button. This logic will include marker placement and map centering.**Tool Command**:

```json
{
  "name": "replace",
  "args": {
    "new_string": "    </div>\n</div>\n\n<script>\n    const maps = {};\n\n    function showStudentMap(id, pLat, pLng, dLat, dLng) {\n        const container = document.getElementById(`map-container-${id}`);\n        if (container.style.display === 'block') {\n            container.style.display = 'none';\n            return;\n        }\n        \n        container.style.display = 'block';\n\n        if (!maps[id]) {\n            const map = L.map(`map-${id}`).setView([pLat, pLng], 13);\n            L.tileLayer('https://{s}.tile.openstreetmap.org/{z}/{x}/{y}.png', {\n                attribution: '© OpenStreetMap contributors'\n            }).addTo(map);\n\n            L.marker([pLat, pLng], {icon: L.icon({\n                iconUrl: 'https://raw.githubusercontent.com/pointhi/leaflet-color-markers/master/img/marker-icon-2x-green.png',\n                shadowUrl: 'https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/leaflet/0.7.7/images/marker-shadow.png',\n                iconSize: [25, 41], iconAnchor: [12, 41], popupAnchor: [1, -34], shadowSize: [41, 41]\n            })}).addTo(map).bindPopup(\"Pickup\");\n\n            L.marker([dLat, dLng], {icon: L.icon({\n                iconUrl: 'https://raw.githubusercontent.com/pointhi/leaflet-color-markers/master/img/marker-icon-2x-red.png',\n                shadowUrl: 'https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/leaflet/0.7.7/images/marker-shadow.png',\n                iconSize: [25, 41], iconAnchor: [12, 41], popupAnchor: [1, -34], shadowSize: [41, 41]\n            })}).addTo(map).bindPopup(\"Drop-off\");\n\n            maps[id] = map;\n            \n            // Fix Leaflet sizing issue when div starts hidden\n            setTimeout(() => { map.invalidateSize(); }, 100);\n        }\n    }\n</script>\n{% endblock %}",
```

## MODEL ✨

I have implemented the **Route Mapping** feature.

**Changes made:**

1.  **Database Update:** Added coordinate fields (`pickup_lat`, `pickup_lng`, `dropoff_lat`, `dropoff_lng`) to the `BookingRequest` model.
2.  **Backend (`app/main.py`):** Added a `/booking/{id}/route` endpoint for instructors to save route data.
3.  **Instructor Dashboard:**
    *   Added a **📍 Route** button for accepted bookings.
    *   Created an interactive **Leaflet.js Map Modal**. Instructors can click on the map to drop a **Green** (Pickup) and **Red** (Drop-off) pin.
    *   Markers are draggable for fine-tuning.
4.  **Student Dashboard:**
    *   Added a **📍 View Route** button for confirmed lessons.
    *   When clicked, an inline map appears showing the pickup and drop-off locations set by the instructor.

**How to verify:**
1.  Login as an **Instructor**.
2.  On an "Accepted" booking, click **"Route"**.
3.  Click twice on the map to set the start and end points, then click **"Save Route"**.
4.  Logout and Login as the **Student**.
5.  On your dashboard, click **"View Route"** to see the pins on your own map.

This completes the features we planned! Is there anything else you'd like to add or adjust?

The server is running. Please try to login again in your browser.

In the meantime, I suspect the `booking_requests` table is missing the new columns (`pickup_lat`, `pickup_lng`, etc.) because `Base.metadata.create_all()` **does not update existing tables**.

I will create a migration script `migrate_db.py` to add these columns manually using SQL. This is the safest fix without deleting your data.**Tool Command**:
```json
{
  "name": "write_file",
  "args": {
    "content": "\nimport sqlite3\n\ndef migrate_database():\n    conn = sqlite3.connect('roadready.db')\n    cursor = conn.cursor()\n    \n    # 1. Add columns to booking_requests\n    columns_to_add = [\n        (\"pickup_lat\", \"FLOAT\"),\n        (\"pickup_lng\", \"FLOAT\"),\n        (\"dropoff_lat\", \"FLOAT\"),\n        (\"dropoff_lng\", \"FLOAT\")\n    ]\n    \n    for col_name, col_type in columns_to_add:\n        try:\n            print(f\"Adding column {col_name}...\")\n            cursor.execute(f\"ALTER TABLE booking_requests ADD COLUMN {col_name} {col_type}\")\n        except sqlite3.OperationalError as e:\n            if \"duplicate column name\" in str(e):\n                print(f\"Column {col_name} already exists.\")\n            else:\n                print(f\"Error adding {col_name}: {e}\")\n\n    # 2. Add columns to instructor_profiles (if we missed any previously)\n    # Checking for availabilities table creation - that is handled by create_all normally, \n    # but let's ensure the relationship works. The table 'instructor_availabilities' should exist.\n    \n    conn.commit()\n    conn.close()\n    print(\"Migration complete.\")\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    migrate_database()\n",
    "file_path": "migrate_db.py"
  }
}
```

## MODEL ✨

The issue is that the `routeModal` initialization: `const routeModal = new bootstrap.Modal(document.getElementById('routeModal'));` runs immediately when the script executes, but it's possible the `bootstrap` library (which is likely loaded in `base.html`) hasn't finished initializing or exposing itself on the window object yet, or the modal element isn't fully ready in the DOM structure expected by Bootstrap at that exact moment.

Also, creating the `new bootstrap.Modal` instance outside of the function scope and reusing it is good practice, but if `bootstrap` isn't defined yet, it will crash the script, causing `openRouteModal` to be undefined when clicked.

I will move the modal initialization *inside* the `openRouteModal` function to ensure everything is loaded when the user clicks the button. I'll also check if `bootstrap` is available.

I'll verify `base.html` first to see where the scripts are loaded.**Tool Command**:
```json
{
  "name": "read_file",
  "args": {
    "file_path": "/home/Hoss/Documents/road-ready/app/templates/base.html"
  }
}
```
