## MODEL ✨

The Bootstrap bundle is loaded at the end of the `body`. The scripts in `instructor_dashboard.html` are inside the `{% block content %}`, which is rendered *before* the Bootstrap script tag in `base.html`.

This explains why `bootstrap` is undefined when the inline script in `instructor_dashboard.html` runs immediately.

I need to wrap the initialization code in `instructor_dashboard.html` within a `window.onload` or wait for the script to load. However, since the function `openRouteModal` is called via `onclick`, it just needs to be defined. The issue is likely the `const routeModal = new bootstrap.Modal(...)` line running before Bootstrap is ready.

I will modify `app/templates/instructor_dashboard.html` to instantiate the modal *lazily* inside the function.**Tool Command**:

## MODEL ✨

I have addressed the issue.

**Problem:** The `bootstrap.Modal` initialization was running immediately when the script was encountered. However, since the script is inside `{% block content %}`, it executes *before* `base.html` loads the Bootstrap library at the end of the `<body>`. This caused a crash (likely `bootstrap is not defined`), preventing `openRouteModal` from working.

**Fix:**
*   Modified `openRouteModal` to initialize the modal instance **lazily** (only when the function is actually called). By the time you click the button, the page and Bootstrap library will have fully loaded.

**Try it again:**
1.  Refresh the Instructor Dashboard.
2.  Click the **"Route"** button on an accepted booking.
3.  The modal should now open, and you should see the map.

Let me know if this resolves it

Steering\", \"D2\": \"Other\", \"D3\": \"Steering Wheel Position\", \"D4\": \"Weight Transfer\",\n    \"E1\": \"Signal\", \"E2\": \"Timing\", \"E3\": \"Cancel\", \"E4\": \"Other\"\n}\n\n# --- Helpers ---\ndef expand_codes(code_string: Optional[str]) -> str:\n    if not code_string:\n        return \"\"\n    codes = [c.strip() for c in code_string.split(',') if c.strip()]\n    expanded = []\n    for code in codes:\n        desc = FAULT_MAP.get(code, code)\n        expanded.append(f\"{code} - {desc}\")\n    return \", \".join(expanded)\n\ndef linkify_chapter(text: str) -> str:\n    if not text: return \"\"\n    import re\n    \n    # Map Chapters to estimated Page Numbers\n    # (In a real app, these would be precise based on the PDF)\n    chapter_map = {\n        \"1\": 5,\n        \"2\": 15,\n        \"3\": 30,\n        \"4\": 50,\n        \"5\": 70,\n        \"6\": 90,\n        \"7\": 110,\n        \"8\": 120\n    }\n    \n    def replace_match(match):\n        full_match = match.group(0) # e.g. (See Chapter 3: Signs...)\n        chapter_num = match.group(1)\n        \n        page = chapter_map.get(chapter_num, 1)\n        \n        # Strip the parens for the link text\n        link_text = full_match.strip(\"()\")\n        \n        return f'(<a href=\"/education/bc?page={page}\" target=\"_blank\" class=\"text-decoration-none\">{link_text} 🔗</a>)'\n\n    # Regex to find \"Chapter X\"\n    # Matches \"(See Chapter X\" or just \"Chapter X\"\n    pattern = r\"\\(See Chapter (\\d+).*?\\)\"\n    return re.sub(pattern, replace_match, text)\n\ntemplates.env.filters[\"expand_codes\"] = expand_codes\ntemplates.env.filters[\"linkify_chapter\"] = linkify_chapter\n\n# Dependency\ndef get_db():\n    db = database.SessionLocal()\n    try:\n        yield db\n    finally:\n        db.close()\n\n# --- Dummy Auth Logic (Cookies for simplicity in prototype) ---\ndef get_current_user_id(request: Request):\n    return request.cookies.get(\"user_id\")\n\ndef get_current_user(request: Request, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):\n    user_id = get_current_user_id(request)\n    if not user_id:\n        return None\n    try:\n        uid = int(user_id)\n        return db.query(models.User).filter(models.User.id == uid).first()\n    except (ValueError, TypeError):\n        return None\n\ndef get_unread_count(db: Session, user: models.User) -> int:\n    if not user: return 0\n    return db.query(models.Message).filter(\n        models.Message.recipient_id == user.id,\n        models.Message.is_read == False\n    ).count()\n\n# --- Routes ---\n\n@app.get(\"/education\", response_class=HTMLResponse)\nasync def education_home(request: Request, user: models.User = Depends(get_current_user), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\"education_home.html\", {\n        \"request\": request,\n        \"user\": user,\n        \"unread_count\": get_unread_count(db, user) if user else 0\n    })\n\n@app.get(\"/education/bc\", response_class=HTMLResponse)\nasync def education_book_bc(request: Request, user: models.User = Depends(get_current_user), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\"education_book.html\", {\n        \"request\": request,\n        \"user\": user,\n        \"unread_count\": get_unread_count(db, user) if user else 0\n    })\n\n@app.get(\"/education/quiz\", response_class=HTMLResponse)\nasync def education_quiz(request: Request, user: models.User = Depends(get_current_user), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):\n    # Fetch 50 random questions\n    from sqlalchemy.sql.expression import func\n    questions = db.query(models.QuizQuestion).order_by(func.random()).limit(50).all()\n    \n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\"quiz.html\", {\n        \"request\": request,\n        \"user\": user,\n        \"questions\": questions,\n        \"unread_count\": get_unread_count(db, user) if user else 0\n    })\n\n@app.post(\"/education/quiz/submit\", response_class=HTMLResponse)\nasync def submit_quiz(\n    request: Request,\n    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n    user: models.User = Depends(get_current_user)\n):\n    form_data = await request.form()\n    \n    score = 0\n    results = []\n    \n    # Process each submitted answer\n    for key, value in form_data.items():\n        if key.startswith(\"q_\"):\n            q_id = int(key.split(\"_\")[1])\n            selected_option = value\n            \n            question = db.query(models.QuizQuestion).filter(models.QuizQuestion.id == q_id).first()\n            if question:\n                is_correct = (selected_option == question.correct_option)\n                if is_correct:\n                    score += 1\n                \n                results.append({\n                    \"question\": question,\n                    \"selected\": selected_option,\n                    \"is_correct\": is_correct\n                })\n    \n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\"quiz_result.html\", {\n        \"request\": request,\n        \"user\": user,\n        \"score\": score,\n        \"total\": len(results),\n        \"results\": results,\n        \"unread_count\": get_unread_count(db, user) if user else 0\n    })\n\n@app.get(\"/\", response_class=HTMLResponse)\nasync def read_root(request: Request, user: models.User = Depends(get_current_user), db: Session = Depends(get_db)):\n    unread = 0\n    if user:\n        try:\n user.reset_token_expiry = (datetime.now() + timedelta(hours=1)).isoformat()\n        db.commit()\n        \n        # Simulate sending email\n        reset_link = db.refresh(new_user)\n    \n    # Redirect to profile setup\n    response = RedirectResponse(url=\"/setup-profile\", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)\n    response.set_cookie(key=\"user_id\", RedirectResponse(url=\"/dashboard\", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)\n\n@app.get(\"/dashboard\", response_class=HTMLResponse)\nasync def dashboard(\n    request: Request, \n    user: models.User = Depends(get_current_user),\n    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n    city: Optional[str] = None,\n    max_price: Optional[float] = None\n):\n    if not user:\n        return RedirectResponse(url=\"/login\")\n    \n    if user.role == \"student\":\n        # Fetch matching instructors\n        query = db.query(models.InstructorProfile).join(models.User).filter(models.InstructorProfile.is_available == True)\n        \n        if city:\n            query = query.filter(models.InstructorProfile.city.ilike(f\"%{city}%\"))\n        \n        if max_price:\n            query = query.filter(models.InstructorProfile.hourly_rate <= max_price)\n            \n        instructors = query.all()\n        \n        # Calculate ratings for display\n        for inst in instructors:\n            reviews = inst.reviews\n            if reviews:\n                avg = sum(r.rating for r in reviews) / len(reviews)\n                inst.average_rating = round(avg, 1)\n                inst.review_count = len(reviews)\n            else:\n                inst.average_rating = None\n                inst.review_count = 0\n\n        # Fetch my booking requests (All history)\n        all_bookings = db.query(models.BookingRequest).filter(models.BookingRequest.student_id == user.id)\\\n            .order_by(models.BookingRequest.date.desc(), models.BookingRequest.time.desc()).all()\n        \n        # Filter Active Bookings (Pending, Accepted, Cancellation Requested)\n        active_bookings = [b for b in all_bookings if b.status in ['pending', 'accepted', 'cancellation_requested']]\n        \n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\"student_dashboard.html\", {\n            \"request\": request, \n            \"user\": user, \n            \"instructors\": instructors,\n            \"active_bookings\": active_bookings,\n            \"filters\": {\"city\": city, \"max_price\": max_price},\n            \"unread_count\": get_unread_count(db, user)\n        })\n    \n    else: # Instructor\n        # Fetch all bookings\n        all_bookings = db.query(models.BookingRequest).filter(\n            models.BookingRequest.instructor_id == user.instructor_profile.id\n        ).order_by(models.BookingRequest.date.desc(), models.BookingRequest.time.desc()).all()\n        \n        active_bookings = [b for b in all_bookings if b.status in ['pending', 'accepted', 'cancellation_requested']]\n        \n        # Calculate my own rating\n        my_reviews = user.instructor_profile.reviews\n        if my_reviews:\n            avg_rating = sum(r.rating for r in my_reviews) / len(my_reviews)\n            avg_rating = round(avg_rating, 1)\n        else:\n            avg_rating = \"New\"\n\n        # Prepare Calendar Events (Accepted Bookings Only)\n        calendar_events = []\n        accepted_bookings = [b for b in all_bookings if b.status == 'accepted']\n        \n        for b in accepted_bookings:\n            try:\n                start_dt = datetime.strptime(f\"{b.date} {b.time}\", \"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M\")\n                end_dt = start_dt + timedelta(hours=b.duration)\n                calendar_events.append({\n                    \"title\": f\"Lesson with {b.student.full_name}\",\n                    \"start\": start_dt.isoformat(),\n                    \"end\": end_dt.isoformat(),\n                    \"color\": \"#198754\" # Bootstrap success green\n                })\n            except ValueError:\n                pass # Skip invalid dates\n\n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\"instructor_dashboard.html\", {\n            \"request\": request, \n            \"user\": user,\n            \"bookings\": active_bookings, # Renamed in template logic context, but variable name passed is what matters\n            \"avg_rating\": avg_rating,\n            \"review_count\": len(my_reviews),\n            \"calendar_events\": json.dumps(calendar_events),\n            \"unread_count\": get_unread_count(db, user)\n        })\n\n@app.get(\"/review/{instructor_id}\", response_class=HTMLResponse)\nasync def review_form(\n    request: Request, \n    instructor_id: int, \n    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n    user: \"unread_count\": get_unread_count(db, user)\n    })\n\n@app.post(\"/review/{instructor_id}\")\nasync def submit_review(\n    request: Request,\n    instructor_id: int,\n    rating: int = Form(...),\n    comment: str = Form(...),\n    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n    user: models.User = Depends(get_current_user)\n):\n    if not user: return RedirectResponse(url=\"/login\")\n    \n    from datetime import datetime\n    \n    review = models.Review(\n        instructor_id=instructor_id,\n        student_id=user.id,\n        rating=rating,\n        comment=comment,\n        created_at=datetime.now().strftime(\"%Y-%m-%d\")\n    )\n    db.add(review)\n    db.commit()\n    \n    return RedirectResponse(url=\"/dashboard\", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)\n\n@app.post(\"/toggle-availability\")\nasync def toggle_availability(\n    request: Request, \n    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n    user: models.User = RedirectResponse(url=\"/dashboard\", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)\n\n@app.post(\"/availability/add\")\nasync def add_availability(\n    request: Request,\n    day_of_week: int = Form(...),\n    start_time: str = Form(...),\n    end_time: str = Form(...),\n    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n    user: models.User = Depends(get_current_user)\n):\n    if not user or user.role != RedirectResponse(url=\"/dashboard\", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)\n\n    availability = models.InstructorAvailability(\n        instructor_id=user.instructor_profile.id,\n        day_of_week=day_of_week,\n        start_time=start_time,\n        end_time=end_time\n    )\n    db.add(availability)\n    db.commit()\n    return RedirectResponse(url=\"/dashboard\", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)\n\n@app.post(\"/availability/delete/{availability_id}\")\nasync def delete_availability(\n    request: Request,\n    availability_id: int,\n    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n    user: models.User = Depends(get_current_user)\n):\n    if not user or user.role != \"instructor\": return RedirectResponse(url=\"/login\")\n    \n    avail = db.query(models.InstructorAvailability).filter(\n        models.InstructorAvailability.id == availability_id,\n        models.InstructorAvailability.instructor_id == user.instructor_profile.id\n    ).first()\n    \n    if avail:\n        db.delete(avail)\n        db.commit()\n        \n    return RedirectResponse(url=\"/dashboard\", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)\n\n@app.get(\"/book/{instructor_id}\", response_class=HTMLResponse)\nasync def booking_form(\n    request: Request, \n    instructor_id: int, \n    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n    user: models.User = Depends(get_current_user)\n):\n    if not user: return RedirectResponse(url=\"/login\")\n    \n    instructor = db.query(models.InstructorProfile).filter(models.InstructorProfile.id == instructor_id).first()\n    if not instructor:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=\"Instructor not found\")\n\n    # Check for existing bookings with this instructor\n    existing_bookings = db.query(models.BookingRequest).filter(\n        models.BookingRequest.student_id == user.id,\n        models.BookingRequest.instructor_id == instructor_id,\n        models.BookingRequest.status.in_([\"pending\", \"accepted\"])\n    ).all()\n        \n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\"booking_form.html\", {\n        \"request\": request, \n        \"user\": user, \n        \"instructor\": instructor,\n        \"existing_bookings\": existing_bookings,\n        \"unread_count\": get_unread_count(db, user)\n    })\n            \n@app.post(\"/book/{instructor_id}\")\nasync def submit_booking(\n    request: Request,\n    instructor_id: int,\n    date: str = Form(...),\n    time: str = Form(...),\n    duration: int = Form(...),\n    pickup_address: str = Form(...),\n    notes: str = Form(\"\"),\n    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n    user: models.User = Depends(get_current_user)\n):\n    if not user: return RedirectResponse(url=\"/login\")\n    \n    # Validation for booking date and time\n    try:\n        new_start = datetime.strptime(f\"{date} {time}\", \"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M\")\n        current_time = datetime.now()\n        \n        instructor = db.query(models.InstructorProfile).filter(models.InstructorProfile.id == instructor_id).first()\n        existing_bookings = db.query(models.BookingRequest).filter(\n            models.BookingRequest.student_id == user.id,\n            models.BookingRequest.instructor_id == instructor_id,\n            models.BookingRequest.status.in_([\"pending\", \"accepted\"])\n        ).all()\n\n        if new_start < current_time:\n            return templates.TemplateResponse(\"booking_form.html\", {\n                \"request\": request,\n                \"user\": user,\n                \"instructor\": instructor,\n                \"existing_bookings\": existing_bookings,\n                \"error_message\": \"Booking date and time cannot be in the past.\",\n                \"unread_count\": get_unread_count(db, user)\n            })\n            \n        if new_start.date() == current_time.date() and new_start < current_time + timedelta(hours=2):\n            return templates.TemplateResponse(\"booking_form.html\", {\n                \"request\": request,\n                \"user\": user,\n                \"instructor\": instructor,\n                \"existing_bookings\": existing_bookings,\n                \"error_message\": \"Same-day bookings must be at least 2 hours in advance.\",\n                \"unread_count\": get_unread_count(db, user)\n            })\n\n    except ValueError:\n        # Handle parsing errors for date/time if necessary\n        instructor = db.query(models.InstructorProfile).filter(models.InstructorProfile.id == instructor_id).first()\n        existing_bookings = db.query(models.BookingRequest).filter(\n            models.BookingRequest.student_id == user.id,\n            models.BookingRequest.instructor_id == instructor_id,\n            models.BookingRequest.status.in_([\"pending\", \"accepted\"])\n        ).all()\n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\"booking_form.html\", {\n            \"request\": request,\n            \"user\": user,\n            \"instructor\": instructor,\n            \"existing_bookings\": existing_bookings,\n            \"error_message\": \"Invalid date or time format.\",\n            \"unread_count\": get_unread_count(db, user)\n        })\n    \n    # Conflict Check Logic\n    try:\n        new_start = datetime.strptime(f\"{date} {time}\", \"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M\")\n        new_end = new_start + timedelta(hours=duration)\n        \n        # 1. Availability Check\n        instructor = db.query(models.InstructorProfile).filter(models.InstructorProfile.id == instructor_id).first()\n        availabilities = instructor.availabilities\n        \n        if availabilities:\n            day_of_week = new_start.weekday() # 0=Monday\n            is_valid_slot = False\n            \n            for slot in availabilities:\n                if slot.day_of_week == day_of_week:\n                    slot_start = datetime.strptime(f\"{date} {slot.start_time}\", \"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M\")\n                    slot_end = datetime.strptime(f\"{date} {slot.end_time}\", \"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M\")\n                    \n                    # Check if booking is fully within the slot\n                    if new_start >= slot_start and new_end <= slot_end:\n                        is_valid_slot = True\n                        break\n            \n            if not is_valid_slot:\n                existing_bookings = db.query(models.BookingRequest).filter(\n                    models.BookingRequest.student_id == user.id,\n                    models.BookingRequest.instructor_id == instructor_id,\n                    models.BookingRequest.status.in_([\"pending\", \"accepted\"])\n                ).all()\n                return templates.TemplateResponse(\"booking_form.html\", {\n                    \"request\": request,\n                    \"user\": user,\n                    \"instructor\": instructor,\n                    \"existing_bookings\": existing_bookings,\n                    \"error_message\": \"The instructor is not available at this time. Please check their working hours.\",\n                    \"unread_count\": get_unread_count(db, user)\n                })\n\n        # 2. db.query(models.Message.sender_id).filter(models.Message.recipient_id == user.id)\n    \n    contact_ids = sent_ids.union(received_ids).all()\n    contact_ids = [i[0] for i in contact_ids]\n    \n db.commit()\n    \n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\"chat.html\", {\n        \"request\": request,\n        \"user\": user,\n        \"other_user\": other_user,\n        \"messages\": models.Message(\n        sender_id=user.id,\n        recipient_id=recipient_id,\n        content=content,\n        timestamp=datetime.now().isoformat()\n    )\n    db.add(msg)\n    db.commit()\n    \n    return RedirectResponse(url=f\"/messages/{recipient_id}\", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)\n"

## MODEL ✨

I will separate the bookings into `incoming_requests` (pending) and `active_appointments` (accepted/cancellation_requested) in `app/main.py`. Then I will update `app/templates/instructor_dashboard.html` to display them in separate cards.

offer a solution for how to verify instructors are certified ?

## MODEL ✨

Verifying instructors is critical for trust and safety. Since government databases (like ICBC or DMVs) rarely have public APIs for privacy reasons, here are the three standard industry approaches, ranging from simple to advanced:

### 1. The "Human-in-the-Loop" Admin Verification (Recommended for MVP)
This is how most platforms (like Uber or Turo) start.
*   **Process:**
    1.  Instructor signs up and **uploads a photo** of their Driving Instructor License and Insurance.
    2.  Their status defaults to `Pending` (Unverified).
    3.  An **Admin** (you) logs into a special dashboard, views the image, checks the name/expiry, and clicks **"Approve"**.
*   **Pros:** Free to implement, high trust (human eye), easy to build.
*   **Cons:** Manual work for the admin.

### 2. Automated ID Verification (Stripe Identity / Onfido)
Integrate a third-party KYC (Know Your Customer) service.
*   **Process:** The app redirects the user to Stripe Identity. They take a selfie and scan their ID. Stripe returns a "Verified" or "Rejected" result via API.
*   **Pros:** Instant, scalable, detects fakes.
*   **Cons:** Costs money per check (~$1.50 - $5.00), requires setting up external accounts.

### 3. Driving School Association Code
If you partner with specific driving schools.
*   **Process:** You generate "School Codes" (e.g., `VALLEY-DRIVING-2024`). Instructors enter this code during signup to auto-verify.
*   **Pros:** Zero manual work.
*   **Cons:** Only works if you have pre-existing partnerships with schools.

---

### My Recommendation: Build Option 1 (Admin Workflow)

I can implement this for you right now. It adds significant realism to your app.

**The Implementation Plan:**
1.  **Database:** Add a `license_image_path` column to the `InstructorProfile`.
2.  **Signup:** Update the Instructor Setup page to accept a **File Upload** (image/pdf) for their license.
3.  **Admin Dashboard:** Create a new route `/admin` (protected by a simple check) where you can see a list of pending instructors, view their license image, and click "Verify".
4.  **Logic:** Update the Instructor Dashboard to show a "Pending Verification" banner until the admin approves them.

I will add a conflict check to the instructor's "Accept" action to prevent double-booking.

Here is the plan:
1.  **Backend (`app/main.py`):** Update the `handle_booking` function.
2.  **Logic:** Before marking a booking as `accepted`, the system will check if it overlaps with any other booking that is *already* `accepted` for that instructor.
3.  **Outcome:** If there is a conflict, the system will block the action and display an error message (I'll render a simple error page or redirect with a query param).

I will update `app/main.py` now.**Tool Command**:
```json
{
  "name": "replace",
  "args": {
    "new_string": "    if action == \"accept\":\n        # Conflict Check: Ensure no other ACCEPTED booking overlaps\n        start_dt = datetime.strptime(f\"{booking.date} {booking.time}\", \"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M\")\n        end_dt = start_dt + timedelta(hours=booking.duration)\n        \n        # Find other accepted bookings for this instructor on the same day\n        concurrent_bookings = db.query(models.BookingRequest).filter(\n            models.BookingRequest.instructor_id == booking.instructor_id,\n            models.BookingRequest.status == \"accepted\",\n            models.BookingRequest.date == booking.date,\n            models.BookingRequest.id != booking.id\n        ).all()\n        \n        for other in concurrent_bookings:\n            other_start = datetime.strptime(f\"{other.date} {other.time}\", \"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M\")\n            other_end = other_start + timedelta(hours=other.duration)\n            \n            # Overlap formula: (StartA < EndB) and (EndA > StartB)\n            if start_dt < other_end and end_dt > other_start:\n                # Conflict found!\n                # Render dashboard with an error message (or simple error page for now)\n                # For a cleaner UI, we can redirect with an error param, but let's just return a template\n                # re-fetching dashboard data is complex here, so let's do a simple error page\n                return templates.TemplateResponse(\"base.html\", {\n                    \"request\": request, \n                    \"user\": user,\n                    \"content\": f\"\"\"\n                        <div class='alert alert-danger'>\n                            <h4>Conflict Detected!</h4>\n                            <p>You cannot accept this booking because it overlaps with another accepted session with <strong>{other.student.full_name}</strong> at {other.time}.</p>\n                            <a href='/dashboard' class='btn btn-primary'>Back to Dashboard</a>\n                        </div>\n                    \"\"\"\n                })\n\n        booking.status = \"accepted\"\n    elif action == \"reject\":\n        booking.status = \"rejected\"\n        \n    db.commit()\n    return RedirectResponse(url=\"/dashboard\", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)\n\n@app.post(\"/booking/{booking_id}/route\")",
```

## MODEL ✨

I have implemented the **Conflict Prevention Logic**.

**How it works:**
1.  When an instructor clicks **"Accept"**, the system now pauses to check their schedule.
2.  It looks for any *already accepted* bookings that overlap with the new request's time slot.
3.  **If there is an overlap:** The system blocks the acceptance and shows a "Conflict Detected" error page, telling the instructor exactly which existing appointment is in the way.
4.  **If the session is cancelled:** The slot becomes free again, and the instructor will be able to accept a new request for that time.

**How to verify:**
1.  As **Student A**, book Instructor X for **10:00 AM** (1 hour).
2.  As **Student B**, book Instructor X for **10:30 AM** (1 hour) on the same day.
3.  Login as **Instructor X**.
4.  Accept **Student A**. (Should succeed).
5.  Try to Accept **Student B**.
    *   **Result:** You should see a "Conflict Detected" error message because 10:30 overlaps with the 10:00-11:00 slot.

## MODEL ✨

I have implemented the **Admin Verification Workflow**.

**Changes made:**

1.  **Database:** Added `license_image` column to `InstructorProfile` (via migration).
2.  **Signup:**
    *   Updated the Instructor Setup form to accept file uploads (Images/PDFs).
    *   Instructors are now created with `is_verified=False` by default.
3.  **Admin Portal:**
    *   Created `/admin` route (accessible to logged-in users for this prototype).
    *   Created `admin_dashboard.html` to list pending instructors.
    *   Admins can view the uploaded ID and click **"Verify"** to approve the instructor.

**How to verify:**
1.  **Register a NEW Instructor account.**
2.  Fill out the setup form and **upload an image** (any dummy image will do).
3.  On your dashboard, you will see "Pending Verification ⏳" (and your status will be Offline/Unavailable).
4.  **Login as another user** (or modify the URL if logged in) and go to `http://localhost:8000/admin`.
5.  You should see the pending instructor in the list.
6.  Click **"View ID"** to check the file.
7.  Click **"Verify"**.
8.  The instructor is now verified and can accept bookings!

This concludes the implementation of the requested features. Is there anything else you need assistance with?

value=str(new_user.id))\n    return response\n\n@app.get(\"/setup-profile\", response_class=HTMLResponse)\nasync def setup_profile_page(request: Request, user: models.User = Depends(get_current_user), RedirectResponse(url=\"/login\")\n    \n    if user.role == \"student\":\n        # Fetch matching instructors\n        query = query.filter(models.InstructorProfile.city.ilike(f\"%{city}%\"))\n        \n        if max_price:\n            query = query.filter(models.InstructorProfile.hourly_rate <= max_price)\n            \n        instructors = query.all()\n        \n        # Calculate ratings for display\n        for inst in instructors:\n            reviews = inst.reviews\n            if reviews:\n                avg = sum(r.rating for r in reviews) / len(reviews)\n                inst.average_rating = round(avg, 1)\n                inst.review_count = len(reviews)\n            else:\n                inst.average_rating = None\n                inst.review_count = 0\n\n        # Fetch my booking requests (All history)\n        all_bookings = db.query(models.BookingRequest).filter(models.BookingRequest.student_id == user.id)\\\n            .order_by(models.BookingRequest.date.desc(), models.BookingRequest.time.desc()).all()\n        \n        # Filter Active Bookings (Pending, Accepted, Cancellation Requested)\n        active_bookings = [b for b in all_bookings if b.status in ['pending', 'accepted', 'cancellation_requested']]\n        \n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\"student_dashboard.html\", {\n            \"request\": request, \n            \"user\": user, \n            \"instructors\": instructors,\n            \"active_bookings\": active_bookings,\n            \"filters\": {\"city\": city, \"max_price\": max_price},\n            \"unread_count\": get_unread_count(db, user)\n        })\n    \n    else: # Instructor\n        # Fetch all bookings\n        all_bookings = db.query(models.BookingRequest).filter(\n            models.BookingRequest.instructor_id == user.instructor_profile.id\n        ).order_by(models.BookingRequest.date.desc(), models.BookingRequest.time.desc()).all()\n        \n        # Split bookings\n        incoming_requests = [b for b in all_bookings if b.status == 'pending']\n        active_appointments = [b for b in all_bookings if b.status in ['accepted', 'cancellation_requested']]\n        \n        # Calculate my own rating\n        my_reviews = user.instructor_profile.reviews\n        if my_reviews:\n            avg_rating = sum(r.rating for r in my_reviews) / len(my_reviews)\n            avg_rating = round(avg_rating, 1)\n        else:\n            avg_rating = \"New\"\n\n        # Prepare Calendar Events (Accepted Bookings Only)\n        calendar_events = []\n        accepted_bookings = [b for b in all_bookings if b.status == 'accepted']\n        \n        for b in accepted_bookings:\n            try:\n                start_dt = datetime.strptime(f\"{b.date} {b.time}\", \"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M\")\n                end_dt = start_dt + timedelta(hours=b.duration)\n                calendar_events.append({\n                    \"title\": f\"Lesson with {b.student.full_name}\",\n                    \"start\": start_dt.isoformat(),\n                    \"end\": end_dt.isoformat(),\n                    \"color\": \"#198754\" # Bootstrap success green\n                })\n            except ValueError:\n                pass # Skip invalid dates\n\n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\"instructor_dashboard.html\", {\n            \"request\": request, \n            \"user\": user,\n            \"incoming_requests\": incoming_requests,\n            \"active_appointments\": active_appointments,\n            \"bookings\": all_bookings, # Keeping strictly for backwards compat if needed, though template will change\n            \"avg_rating\": avg_rating,\n            \"review_count\": len(my_reviews),\n            \"calendar_events\": json.dumps(calendar_events),\n            \"unread_count\": get_unread_count(db, user)\n        })\n\n@app.get(\"/review/{instructor_id}\", response_class=HTMLResponse)\nasync def review_form(\n    request: Request, \n    instructor_id: int, rating=rating,\n        comment=comment,\n        created_at=datetime.now().strftime(\"%Y-%m-%d\")\n    )\n    db.add(review)\n    db.commit()\n    \n    return RedirectResponse(url=\"/dashboard\", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)\n\n@app.post(\"/toggle-availability\")\nasync def toggle_availability(\n    request: Request, \n    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n    user: models.User = RedirectResponse(url=\"/dashboard\", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)\n\n@app.post(\"/availability/add\")\nasync def add_availability(\n    request: Request,\n    day_of_week: int = Form(...),\n    start_time: str = Form(...),\n    end_time: str = Form(...),\n    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n    user: models.User = Depends(get_current_user)\n):\n    if not user or user.role != RedirectResponse(url=\"/dashboard\", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)\n\n    availability = models.InstructorAvailability(\n        instructor_id=user.instructor_profile.id,\n ).first()\n    \n    if avail:\n        db.delete(avail)\n        db.commit()\n        \n    return RedirectResponse(url=\"/dashboard\", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)\n\n@app.get(\"/book/{instructor_id}\", response_class=HTMLResponse)\nasync def booking_form(\n    request: Request, \n    instructor_id: int, \n    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n    user: models.User = Depends(get_current_user)\n):\n    if not user: return RedirectResponse(url=\"/login\")\n    \n    instructor = db.query(models.InstructorProfile).filter(models.InstructorProfile.id == instructor_id).first()\n    if not instructor:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=\"Instructor not found\")\n\n    # Check for existing bookings with this instructor\n    existing_bookings = db.query(models.BookingRequest).filter(\n        models.BookingRequest.student_id == user.id,\n        models.BookingRequest.instructor_id == instructor_id,\n        models.BookingRequest.status.in_([\"pending\", \"accepted\"])\n    ).all()\n        \n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\"booking_form.html\", {\n        \"request\": request, \n        \"user\": user, \n        \"instructor\": instructor,\n        \"existing_bookings\": existing_bookings,\n        \"unread_count\": get_unread_count(db, user)\n    })\n            \n@app.post(\"/book/{instructor_id}\")\nasync def submit_booking(\n    request: Request,\n    instructor_id: int,\n    date: str = Form(...),\n    time: str = Form(...),\n    duration: int = Form(...),\n    pickup_address: str = Form(...),\n    notes: str = Form(\"\"),\n    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n    user: models.User = Depends(get_current_user)\n):\n    if not user: return RedirectResponse(url=\"/login\")\n    \n    # Validation for booking date and time\n    try:\n        new_start = datetime.strptime(f\"{date} {time}\", \"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M\")\n        current_time = datetime.now()\n        \n        instructor = db.query(models.InstructorProfile).filter(models.InstructorProfile.id == instructor_id).first()\n        existing_bookings = db.query(models.BookingRequest).filter(\n            models.BookingRequest.student_id == user.id,\n            models.BookingRequest.instructor_id == instructor_id,\n            models.BookingRequest.status.in_([\"pending\", \"accepted\"])\n        ).all()\n\n        if new_start < current_time:\n            return templates.TemplateResponse(\"booking_form.html\", {\n                \"request\": request,\n                \"user\": user,\n                \"instructor\": instructor,\n                \"existing_bookings\": existing_bookings,\n                \"error_message\": \"Booking date and time cannot be in the past.\",\n                \"unread_count\": get_unread_count(db, user)\n            })\n            \n        if new_start.date() == current_time.date() and new_start < current_time + timedelta(hours=2):\n            return templates.TemplateResponse(\"booking_form.html\", {\n                \"request\": request,\n                \"user\": user,\n                \"instructor\": instructor,\n                \"existing_bookings\": existing_bookings,\n                \"error_message\": \"Same-day bookings must be at least 2 hours in advance.\",\n                \"unread_count\": get_unread_count(db, user)\n            })\n\n    except ValueError:\n        # Handle parsing errors for date/time if necessary\n        instructor = db.query(models.InstructorProfile).filter(models.InstructorProfile.id == instructor_id).first()\n        existing_bookings = db.query(models.BookingRequest).filter(\n            models.BookingRequest.student_id == user.id,\n            models.BookingRequest.instructor_id == instructor_id,\n            models.BookingRequest.status.in_([\"pending\", \"accepted\"])\n        ).all()\n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\"booking_form.html\", {\n            \"request\": request,\n            \"user\": user,\n            \"instructor\": instructor,\n            \"existing_bookings\": existing_bookings,\n            \"error_message\": \"Invalid date or time format.\",\n            \"unread_count\": get_unread_count(db, user)\n        })\n    \n    # Conflict Check Logic\n    try:\n        new_start = datetime.strptime(f\"{date} {time}\", \"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M\")\n        new_end = new_start + timedelta(hours=duration)\n        \n        # 1. Availability Check\n        instructor = db.query(models.InstructorProfile).filter(models.InstructorProfile.id == instructor_id).first()\n        availabilities = instructor.availabilities\n        \n        if availabilities:\n            day_of_week = new_start.weekday() # 0=Monday\n            is_valid_slot = False\n            \n            for slot in availabilities:\n                if slot.day_of_week == day_of_week:\n                    slot_start = datetime.strptime(f\"{date} {slot.start_time}\", \"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M\")\n                    slot_end = datetime.strptime(f\"{date} {slot.end_time}\", \"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M\")\n                    \n                    # Check if booking is fully within the slot\n                    if new_start >= slot_start and new_end <= slot_end:\n                        is_valid_slot = True\n                        break\n            \n            if not is_valid_slot:\n                existing_bookings = db.query(models.BookingRequest).filter(\n                    models.BookingRequest.student_id == user.id,\n                    models.BookingRequest.instructor_id == instructor_id,\n                    models.BookingRequest.status.in_([\"pending\", \"accepted\"])\n                ).all()\n                return templates.TemplateResponse(\"booking_form.html\", {\n                    \"request\": request,\n                    \"user\": user,\n                    \"instructor\": instructor,\n                    \"existing_bookings\": existing_bookings,\n                    \"error_message\": \"The instructor is not available at this time. Please check their working hours.\",\n                    \"unread_count\": get_unread_count(db, user)\n                })\n\n        # 2. Check against existing ACCEPTED bookings for this instructor\n        existing_accepted = db.query(models.BookingRequest).filter(\n            models.BookingRequest.instructor_id == instructor_id,\n            models.BookingRequest.status == \"accepted\",\n            models.BookingRequest.date == date\n        ).all()\n        \n        for booking in existing_accepted:\n            b_start = datetime.strptime(f\"{booking.date} {booking.time}\", \"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M\")\n            b_end = b_start + timedelta(hours=booking.duration)\n            \n            # Check for overlap: (StartA < EndB) and (EndA > StartB)\n            if new_start < b_end and new_end > b_start:\n                 # Fetch instructor and existing bookings again for re-rendering the form\n                instructor = db.query(models.InstructorProfile).filter(models.InstructorProfile.id == instructor_id).first()\n                existing_bookings = db.query(models.BookingRequest).filter(\n                    models.BookingRequest.student_id == user.id,\n                    models.BookingRequest.instructor_id == instructor_id,\n                    models.BookingRequest.status.in_([\"pending\", \"accepted\"])\n                ).all()\n                \n                return templates.TemplateResponse(\"booking_form.html\", {\n                    \"request\": request,\n                    \"user\": user,\n                    \"instructor\": instructor,\n                    \"existing_bookings\": existing_bookings,\n                    \"error_message\": \"The instructor is not available at the selected time. Please choose a different slot.\",\n                    \"unread_count\": get_unread_count(db, user)\n                })\n\n    except ValueError:\n        pass # Handle parsing errors if necessary\n\n    booking = models.BookingRequest(\n        student_id=user.id,\n        instructor_id=instructor_id,\n        date=date,\n        time=time,\n        duration=duration,\n        pickup_address=pickup_address,\n        notes=notes\n    )\n    db.add(booking)\n    db.commit()\n    \n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\"booking_success.html\", {\n        \"request\": request,\n        \"user\": user,\n        \"unread_count\": get_unread_count(db, user)\n    })\n\n@app.post(\"/booking/{booking_id}/{action}\")\nasync def handle_booking(\n    request: Request,\n    booking_id: int,\n    action: str,\n    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n    user: models.User = Depends(get_current_user)\n):\n    if not user or user.role != \"instructor\": return RedirectResponse(url=\"/login\")\n    \n    booking = db.query(models.BookingRequest).filter(models.BookingRequest.id == booking_id).first()\n    \n    # Ensure this booking belongs to the logged-in instructor\n    if not booking or booking.instructor.user_id != user.id:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=\"Not authorized\")\n        \n    if action == \"accept\":\n        # Conflict Check: Ensure no other ACCEPTED booking overlaps\n        start_dt = datetime.strptime(f\"{booking.date} {booking.time}\", \"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M\")\n        end_dt = start_dt + timedelta(hours=booking.duration)\n        \n        # Find other accepted bookings for this instructor on the same day\n        concurrent_bookings = db.query(models.BookingRequest).filter(\n            models.BookingRequest.instructor_id == booking.instructor_id,\n            models.BookingRequest.status == \"accepted\",\n            models.BookingRequest.date == booking.date,\n            models.BookingRequest.id != booking.id\n        ).all()\n        \n        for other in concurrent_bookings:\n            other_start = datetime.strptime(f\"{other.date} {other.time}\", \"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M\")\n            other_end = other_start + timedelta(hours=other.duration)\n            \n            # Overlap formula: (StartA < EndB) and (EndA > StartB)\n            if start_dt < other_end and end_dt > other_start:\n                # Conflict found!\n                # Render dashboard with an error message (or simple error page for now)\n                # For a cleaner UI, we can redirect with an error param, but let's just return a template\n                # re-fetching dashboard data is complex here, so let's do a simple error page\n                return templates.TemplateResponse(\"base.html\", {\n                    \"request\": request, \n                    \"user\": user,\n                    \"content\": f\"\"\"\n                        <div class='alert alert-danger'>\n                            <h4>Conflict Detected!</h4>\n                            <p>You cannot accept this booking because it overlaps with another accepted session with <strong>{other.student.full_name}</strong> at {other.time}.</p>\n                            <a href='/dashboard' class='btn btn-primary'>Back to Dashboard</a>\n                        </div>\n                    \"\"\"\n                })\n\n        booking.status = \"accepted\"\n    elif action == \"reject\":\n        booking.status = \"rejected\"\n        \n    db.commit()\n    return RedirectResponse(url=\"/dashboard\", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)\n\n@app.post(\"/booking/{booking_id}/route\")\nasync def update_booking_route(\n    booking_id: int,\n    pickup_lat: float = Form(...),\n    pickup_lng: float = Form(...),\n    dropoff_lat: float = Form(...),\n    dropoff_lng: float = Form(...),\n    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n    user: models.User = Depends(get_current_user)\n):\n    if not user or user.role != \"instructor\": return RedirectResponse(url=\"/login\")\n    \n    booking = db.query(models.BookingRequest).filter(models.BookingRequest.id == booking_id).first()\n    if not booking or booking.instructor.user_id != user.id:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=\"Not authorized\")\n        \n    booking.pickup_lat = pickup_lat\n    booking.pickup_lng = pickup_lng\n    booking.dropoff_lat = dropoff_lat\n    booking.dropoff_lng = dropoff_lng\n    \n    db.commit()\n    return RedirectResponse(url=\"/dashboard\", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)\n\n@app.get(\"/log-session/{booking_id}\", response_class=HTMLResponse)\nasync def log_session_form(\n    request: Request, \n    booking_id: int, \n    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n    user: models.User = Depends(get_current_user)\n):\n    if not user or user.role != \"instructor\": return RedirectResponse(url=\"/login\")\n    \n    booking = db.query(models.BookingRequest).filter(models.BookingRequest.id == booking_id).first()\n    if not booking or booking.instructor.user_id != user.id:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=\"Not authorized\")\n    \n    if booking.status == \"completed\":\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=\"Session already completed. Cannot log again.\")\n        \n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\"log_session_form.html\", {\n        \"request\": request,\n        \"user\": user,\n        \"booking\": booking,\n        \"fault_map\": FAULT_MAP,\n        \"unread_count\": get_unread_count(db, user)\n    })\n\n@app.post(\"/log-session/{booking_id}\")\nasync def submit_session_log(\n    request: Request,\n    booking_id: int,\n    duration: int = Form(...),\n    weather: str = Form(...),\n    road_type: str = Form(...),\n    shared_feedback: str = Form(...),\n    private_notes: str = Form(\"\"),\n    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n    user: models.User = Depends(get_current_user)\n):\n    if not user or user.role != \"instructor\": return RedirectResponse(url=\"/login\")\n    \n    booking = db.query(models.BookingRequest).filter(models.BookingRequest.id == booking_id).first()\n    if not booking or booking.instructor.user_id != user.id:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=\"Not authorized\")\n        \n    if booking.status == \"completed\":\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=\"Session already completed. Cannot log again.\")\n    \n    # Process dynamic fault counts\n    form_data = await request.form()\n    \n    obs_list = []\n    space_list = []\n    speed_list = []\n    steering_list = []\n    communication_list = []\n    \n    for key, value in form_data.items():\n        if key.startswith(\"counts_\"):\n            code = key.split(\"_\")[1] # Extract code e.g. \"A1\"\n            try:\n                count = int(value)\n            except ValueError:\n                count = 0\n            \n            if count > 0:\n                # Add code 'count' times to the list\n                faults = [code] * count\n                \n                if code.startswith(\"A\"):\n                    obs_list.extend(faults)\n                elif code.startswith(\"B\"):\n                    space_list.extend(faults)\n                elif code.startswith(\"C\"):\n                    speed_list.extend(faults)\n                elif code.startswith(\"D\"):\n                    steering_list.extend(faults)\n                elif code.startswith(\"E\"):\n                    communication_list.extend(faults)\n\n    session = models.DrivingSession(\n        booking_id=booking_id,\n        duration_minutes=duration,\n        weather_condition=weather,\n        road_type=road_type,\n        observation_data=\",\".join(obs_list),\n        space_margin_data=\",\".join(space_list),\n        speed_data=\",\".join(speed_list),\n        steering_data=\",\".join(steering_list),\n        communication_data=\",\".join(communication_list),\n        shared_feedback=shared_feedback,\n        instructor_private_notes=private_notes,\n        created_at=datetime.now().strftime(\"%Y-%m-%d\")\n    )\n    \n    db.add(session)\n    \n    # Mark booking as completed\n    booking = db.query(models.BookingRequest).filter(models.BookingRequest.id == booking_id).first()\n    if booking:\n        booking.status = \"completed\"\n        \n    db.commit()\n    return RedirectResponse(url=\"/dashboard\", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)\n\n@app.get(\"/progress\", response_class=HTMLResponse)\nasync def student_progress(\n    request: Request,\n    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n    user: models.User = Depends(get_current_user)\n):\n    if not user or user.role != \"student\": return RedirectResponse(url=\"/login\")\n    \n    # Fetch all bookings for history (Merged view)\n    bookings = db.query(models.BookingRequest).filter(models.BookingRequest.student_id == user.id)\\\n        .order_by(models.BookingRequest.date.desc(), models.BookingRequest.time.desc()).all()\n\n    # Fetch sessions for stats (Keep existing logic or derive from bookings)\n    # Existing logic joins BookingRequest. Let's keep it to ensure we only count legitimate sessions.\n    sessions = db.query(models.DrivingSession).join(models.BookingRequest).filter(\n        models.BookingRequest.student_id == user.id\n    ).all()\n    \n    #   if formatted_list:\n            grouped_faults[cat_name] = formatted_list\n    \n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\"instructor_progress.html\", {\n        \"request\": request,\n        \"user\": user,\n        \"bookings\": bookings,\n        \"students\": students,\n        \"selected_student_id\": student_id,\n        \"total_earnings\": total_earnings,\n        \"stats\": {\n            \"total_lessons\": total_lessons,\n            \"total_hours\": total_hours\n        },\n        \"grouped_faults\": grouped_faults,\n        \"unread_count\": get_unread_count(db, user)\n    })\n\n# --- Cancellation Routes ---\n\n@app.post(\"/cancel-booking/{booking_id}\")\nasync def cancel_booking(\n    request: Request,\n    booking_id: int,\n    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n    user: models.User = Depends(get_current_user)\n):\n    if not user: return RedirectResponse(url=\"/login\")\n    \n    booking = db.query(models.BookingRequest).filter(models.BookingRequest.id == booking_id).first()\n    if not booking or booking.student_id != user.id:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=\"Not authorized\")\n        \n    if booking.status == \"completed\":\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=\"Cannot cancel a completed session.\")\n        \n    # Check 24h rule\n    try:\n        booking_dt = datetime.strptime(f\"{booking.date} {booking.time}\", \"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M\")\n        time_diff = booking_dt - datetime.now()\n        \n        # Simple penalty logic (in real app, charge user)\n        if time_diff < timedelta(hours=24):\n            print(f\"PENALTY: Student {user.email} cancelled booking {booking_id} within 24h. 25% fee applies.\")\n            \n        booking.status = \"cancelled\"\n        db.commit()\n        \n    except ValueError:\n        pass \n        \n    return RedirectResponse(url=\"/dashboard\", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)\n\n@app.post(\"/request-cancellation/{booking_id}\")\nasync def request_cancellation(\n    request: Request,\n    booking_id: int,\n    db: Session = detail=\"Not authorized\")\n    \n    if booking.status == \"completed\":\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=\"Cannot request cancellation for a completed session.\")\n        \n    if booking.status == \"accepted\":\n        booking.status = \"cancellation_requested\"\n        db.commit()\n        \n    return RedirectResponse(url=\"/dashboard\", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)\n\n@app.post(\"/respond-cancellation/{booking_id}/{action}\")\nasync def respond_cancellation(\n    request: Request,\n    booking_id: int,\n    action: str,\n authorized\")\n        \n    if action == \"approve\":\n        booking.status = \"cancelled\"\n    elif action == \"deny\":\n        booking.status = \"accepted\"\n        \n    db.commit()\n db.query(models.Message.recipient_id).filter(models.Message.sender_id == user.id)\n    # IDs of people I received from\n    received_ids = db.query(models.Message.sender_id).filter(models.Message.recipient_id == user.id)\n    \n    contact_ids = sent_ids.union(received_ids).all()\n    contact_ids = [i[0] for i in contact_ids]\n    \n    contacts = db.query(models.User).filter(models.User.id.in_(contact_ids)).all()\n    \n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\"messages_list.html\", {\n        \"request\": request,\n        \"user\": user,\n        \"contacts\": contacts,\n        \"unread_count\": get_unread_count(db, user)\n    })\n\n@app.get(\"/messages/{other_user_id}\", response_class=HTMLResponse)\nasync def chat_view(\n    request: Request,\n    other_user_id: int,\n    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n    user: models.User = Depends(get_current_user)\n):\n    if not user: return RedirectResponse(url=\"/login\")\n    \n    other_user = db.query(models.User).filter(models.User.id == other_user_id).first()\n    if not other_user:\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=\"User not found\")\n        \n    # Fetch conversation\n    from sqlalchemy import or_, and_\n    messages = db.query(models.Message).filter(\n        or_(\n            and_(models.Message.sender_id == user.id, models.Message.recipient_id == other_user_id),\n            and_(models.Message.sender_id == other_user_id, models.Message.recipient_id == user.id)\n        )\n    ).order_by(models.Message.timestamp.asc()).all()\n    \n    # Mark as read\n    for m in messages:\n        if m.recipient_id == user.id and not m.is_read:\n            m.is_read = True\n    db.commit()\n    \n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\"chat.html\", {\n        \"request\": request,\n        \"user\": user,\n        \"other_user\": other_user,\n        \"messages\": messages,\n        \"unread_count\": get_unread_count(db, user)\n    })\n\n@app.post(\"/send-message\")\nasync def send_message(\n    request: Request,\n    recipient_id: int = Form(...),\n    content: str = Form(...),\n    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n    user: models.User = Depends(get_current_user)\n):\n    if not user: return RedirectResponse(url=\"/login\")\n    \n    msg = models.Message(\n        sender_id=user.id,\n        recipient_id=recipient_id,\n        content=content,\n        timestamp=datetime.now().isoformat()\n    )\n    db.add(msg)\n    db.commit()\n    \n    return RedirectResponse(url=f\"/messages/{recipient_id}\", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)\n"

```json
    "new_string": "    db.add(profile)\n    db.commit()\n    return RedirectResponse(url=\"/dashboard\", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)\n\n# --- Admin Routes ---\n\n@app.get(\"/admin\", response_class=HTMLResponse)\nasync def admin_dashboard(\n    request: Request,\n    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n    user: models.User = Depends(get_current_user)\n):\n    # Simple security check: In real app, check user.role == 'admin'\n    # For prototype, we'll assume anyone who knows this URL is admin (or add a hardcoded check)\n    if not user: return RedirectResponse(url=\"/login\")\n    \n    # Fetch pending instructors\n    pending_instructors = db.query(models.InstructorProfile).filter(models.InstructorProfile.is_verified == False).all()\n    \n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\"admin_dashboard.html\", {\n        \"request\": request,\n        \"user\": user,\n        \"pending_instructors\": pending_instructors\n    })\n\n@app.post(\"/admin/verify/{profile_id}\")\nasync def admin_verify_instructor(\n    request: Request,\n    profile_id: int,\n    db: Session = Depends(get_db),\n    user: models.User = Depends(get_current_user)\n):\n    if not user: return RedirectResponse(url=\"/login\")\n    \n    profile = db.query(models.InstructorProfile).filter(models.InstructorProfile.id == profile_id).first()\n    if profile:\n        profile.is_verified = True\n        db.commit()\n        \n    return RedirectResponse(url=\"/admin\", status_code=status.HTTP_303_SEE_OTHER)\n\n@app.post(\"/setup-student\")",
```

## USER 🧑‍💻

how far are we until we can launch the project? give me a percentage

---

## MODEL ✨

That is a great question. Here is an honest assessment of where "Road Ready" stands:

### 🏁 Feature Completeness: **95%**
Functionally, the app is excellent. It has a verified workflow, complex booking logic with conflict detection, interactive maps, data visualization, and educational content. As a **demo or MVP (Minimum Viable Product)** to show investors or friends, it is ready.

### 🚀 Production Launch Readiness: **50%**
To let **real strangers** sign up and use it on the open internet, we are about halfway there. The "unsexy" but critical infrastructure and security work is missing.

---

### The "Road to 100%" Checklist

Here is exactly what separates the current prototype from a launchable product:

#### 1. Security (CRITICAL) - 🛑 Blocker
*   **Password Hashing:** Currently, passwords are stored in **plain text**. If the database leaks, everyone is compromised. We *must* implement hashing (using `bcrypt` or `argon2`).
*   **HTTPS:** You need an SSL certificate (standard for any web host).
*   **Session Security:** Replace the simple cookie logic with secure, signed session tokens (JWT or server-side sessions).

#### 2. Infrastructure & Data
*   **Database:** We are using **SQLite** (a file). It works great for dev, but for a real app with concurrent users, we should switch to **PostgreSQL**.
*   **File Storage:** Uploaded licenses are saved to the local server disk. In production (like on Heroku or AWS), these files will vanish when the server restarts. We need **AWS S3** or Google Cloud Storage.

#### 3. Real-World Integrations
*   **Payments:** Currently, payment is "honor system". Integrating **Stripe** to take a deposit or full payment would be the monetization key.
*   **Emails:** "Forgot Password" currently prints to the console. We need to connect **SendGrid** or **AWS SES** to actually email the users.

#### 4. Legal & Compliance
*   **Terms & Privacy:** You need legal text covering liability (especially since this involves driving).

---

### My Recommendation

If you want to move towards a real launch, we should tackle **Security** first.

**Shall I secure the application by implementing Password Hashing (bcrypt)?** This will require resetting the current user passwords (or migrating them), but it's the most important step for a real app.
